Design Doc · Product + Architecture

STRYKE v1
— Design

iOS-first soccer shooting analysis: one phone, a session of shots, one thing to change.

R1In Review
Date
2026-08-28
Authored by
6 Opus authors · 6 adversarial critics · 1 synthesizer
Scope
mobile · be-api · be-vision · CV · rankings
Pairs with
Progress hub

Single source of truth for STRYKE v1. This document is the merge of 01-product.md, 02-mobile-architecture.md, 03-cv-pipeline-and-calibration.md, 04-backend-data-apis.md, 05-failures-and-plan.md and 06-ui-design-direction.md, against BRIEF.md. Where two sections disagreed, this document picks one and says why in a > Resolved: callout. Nothing is left as "both are true".

Provenance labels are mandatory and are used throughout, because the recurring bug class in this codebase is a default or a target read as a measurement (source: ~/Desktop/programming/Dev-Agentic/TBG-DOCS/plans/24-stryke-match-analytics/README.md — four instances in one afternoon):

Tag Meaning
measured Quoted from a file that measured it, cited inline
derived Computed here from a stated formula over stated MEASURED inputs
target A number nothing has hit yet, with the work that would hit it named
unmeasured / proposed An engineering estimate, with the task that replaces it named

Executive summary

  1. What we build first: a session-shaped iOS capture app — shoot 10–20 times without touching the phone, every shot accumulates as a 3D arc on one map, tap one to get the video, the numbers, your body at impact, and one thing to change.
  2. The unit of value is the session, not the clip. That single reframing is what separates STRYKE from HeroShot, and it drives the capture ring, the two-lane analysis, the shot map, and the multi-shot coaching engine.
  3. P0 = capture + shot map + shot detail + confidence surfaces + JK layer + multi-shot coaching. Percentiles, leaderboards, keeper simulation, clip import and any point-value km/h are not P0.
  4. Hard gate 1 — radar before rankings. Speed and placement error must be validated against a Pocket Radar and a physically measured goal (BRIEF); until 03 §6.5 A1–A13 pass on Run 2, cohort_percentile.is_published stays false everywhere and config.point_speed_released stays false.
  5. Hard gate 2 — licence before any build leaves the team. GVHMR is non-commercial and SMPL/SMPL-X is MPI research-only. No external TestFlight, no investor device, no App Store submission until a Meshcapade licence is signed and GVHMR's code question is answered in writing, or the SAM-3D-Body / GEM-X migration is merged and green.
  6. Every speed on every surface is a band at P0. speed_band_kmh is "ALWAYS present. This is the product" (stryke-pipeline/docs/BALL.md); speed_kmh is null unless the pipeline claims it, and even then the app must AND in a server-owned release flag.
  7. The measured numbers this design rests on: ball lane ~17 s CPU for a 5 s / 264-frame clip; GVHMR model stage 0.63 s but preprocess 39.3 s and model load 17.9–25 s; hero render ~30 s for two styles / 117 s cold; ~199 s measured cold end-to-end (source: …/23-stryke-heroshoot/README.md §3 and its 2026-08-13 / 2026-08-19 logs). The widely-quoted "~25 s warm" is a target, not a measurement.
  8. Therefore: renders are lazy (S7 runs on tap), the ball and pose lanes run in parallel, and the arc lands on the map ~20 s after upload completes while the mesh arrives later. partial is the normal state, not a degraded one.
  9. Accuracy is a scale problem, not a tracking problem: depth error transfers to speed 1:1, in-plane speed contributes < 1 %, and the ball-diameter ruler carries 24–28 % relative depth error (source: …/23-stryke-heroshoot/ball-accuracy-upgrade.md §1–§2). Focal alone spans 44.1 → 104.6 km/h on one real clip, f = 500 vs 1300 px (source: …/stryke-pipeline/out/goal_anchored/scene_measured.json).
  10. The only measured band on a real clip today is [28.8, 76.6] km/h — after the gravity ruler was deleted on 2026-08-21 (commit 36d6aa0) because solved g_eff came back at 14.69 m/s² (source: ~/Desktop/programming/stryke-pipeline/docs/BALL.md). Two anchors remain: goal PnP and ball diameter.
  11. The arc's shape is ambiguous: 4 camera basins within 0.5 px RMS, spanning 7.7–26.2° elevation and 0.14–0.71 m apex (source: …/23-stryke-heroshoot/README.md log 2026-08-19; ~/Desktop/programming/stryke-pipeline/docs/BALL.md). So apex, elevation and azimuth are not rendered, and basin_ambiguous draws every surviving arc as a band.
  12. What we can claim today, and build the P0 screens around: session-relative ranking ("your hardest strike today") and per-metric deltas — correlation survives a scale error — Athla ICC 0.970 with a +6.8 % bias (source: …/23-stryke-heroshoot/ball-accuracy-upgrade.md §2).
  13. Verified Ranked Mode exists to remove the scale unknowns: the ARKit ritual fixes camera height, tilt and focal, dropping the fit from 7 free parameters to 3 (+1 curl) — the direct fix for the basin degeneracy (source: …/23-stryke-heroshoot/ball-accuracy-upgrade.md T3.2).
  14. Coaching never fires on one shot: 7 eligible shots in one comparison cell, a one-sided exact binomial against p₀ = 0.30 with Holm at α/8 = 0.00625, one issue at a time, then a 3-eligible-shot verification. "6 of 7" clears; "5 of 7" does not.
  15. Timeline: ~20 calendar weeks to a rankings launch (17 weeks of critical path + 15 % stated slack), with the radar ordered in week 1 so it costs zero critical-path weeks, and ~27 weeks if rankings are made to wait for the Stage 5 depth ruler.

01 Product requirements (concise)

1.1 Problem, users, outcome

A player who wants to strike a ball better has two options today and both fail. Film yourself and you get a video that cannot tell you whether this shot was faster than the last one, where in the goal it arrived, or which of the twelve things your body did cost you the result. Buy a launch monitor and you get accuracy that costs thousands, lives in one place, and reports the ball's numbers without reporting what your body did to produce them.

The gap is not "measure the ball". It is attributing a change in result to a change in form, across repeated attempts, on a normal pitch, with one phone — which needs ball flight and body motion and a stable frame of reference and enough shots to see a pattern.

Segment Who they are What they do today What STRYKE gives them
Primary — the repeat striker 15–30, plays weekly, practises shooting alone or with one friend Phone propped on a bag, watches the clip once, learns nothing A session where 20 shots accumulate into one picture, and one thing to change
Secondary — the competitive amateur Same, but wants a number to beat and a board to be on Argues about who hits it harder A registered field where records are comparable, and a JK record to chase
Tertiary — the coach / academy Runs small-group shooting sessions Eyeballs it A per-player session history under identical conditions

A player finishes a 20-minute session and leaves with: every shot on one 3D map, one shot inspected frame-by-frame with their own body in 3D, one specific thing to change next time, and evidence from the next three eligible shots about whether the change worked.

Not in v1: match play (workstream 24 — …/24-stryke-match-analytics/README.md), goalkeepers as users, multi-person capture.

1.2 The clip ↔ shot contract

A session records one continuous clip on the device's ring, and the device cuts one 3.5 s clip per detected shot — window [T − 1.0 s, T + 2.5 s], IDR-aligned, sample-passthrough, no re-encode (02 §3.2). One shot, one uploaded artifact. There is no 3-shots-per-clip cap in STRYKE: the "최대 3회" line in UploadGuidelines.tsx is a HeroShot upload-form constraint and survives only on the P1 photo-library import path, where the server does receive a multi-shot file (04 §4.3).

Resolved

Resolved: who segments a session into shots. 02 §3 says the device owns segmentation and 03 §1.3 S1 becomes a validator; 03 §1.3 S1 specifies a server producer; 04 §4.3 says server-side segmentation is not on the v1 critical path; 01 §7.2 lists S1 as a P0 engineering dependency. The device produces the windows; server-side S1 is a validator of them. The device is the only place with the audio channel, the IMU, the resting-ball ROI and a retroactive ring, and analyze_frames (ball.py:1819) finds exactly one contact per video today, so a server producer is net-new work the device makes unnecessary. S1 as a producer stays specced for capture_source='photo_library' imports (P1) and nowhere else. The P0 engineering dependency is therefore the device detector (02 §3.1) plus the server-side window validator, not a new GPU stage.

1.3 Non-goals for v1

Non-goal Why
Quoting an absolute point-value km/h The radar ground-truth set is built during v1 (Stage 1), but until 03 §6.5 A1–A5/A11 pass no accuracy claim is falsifiable — not one of ~40 surveyed repos/papers reports a launch-speed error vs radar (source: …/ball-accuracy-upgrade.md §5). Until config.point_speed_released flips, every speed on every screen is a band
Global percentiles and public leaderboards Cohort keys (distance × goal × shot type × foot × age) shatter the population; the publish rule is far above launch volume
Named real goalkeepers Likeness + motion rights unowned (BRIEF §8)
Spin magnitude, apex height in metres, elevation angle as measurements Forbidden by the pipeline's own contract: claims.spin_magnitude is hard-wired false; competing camera basins differ by < 2 px RMS while disagreeing 82 % on apex (source: …/docs/BALL.md)
Live / on-device real-time analysis Measured surrounding pipeline is ~199 s cold end-to-end; the ~25 s warm figure is an unshipped TARGET
Android iOS-first: exact per-frame intrinsics via AVCaptureConnection.isCameraIntrinsicMatrixDeliveryEnabled and ARKit gravity-aligned pose are the whole reason capture-side accuracy is reachable (source: …/ball-accuracy-upgrade.md T3.1/T3.2). Camera2's LENS_INTRINSIC_CALIBRATION is optional per device

1.4 Success metrics

Every metric is instrumented on a named event. No vanity metrics.

# Metric Target (launch + 60 days) Event / source
M1 Session depth — median shots per completed session ≥ 8 session_completed.shot_count
M2a Shot-to-arc, p50 — last frame → arc on the shot map (partial) ≤ 30 s, warm pool, Wi-Fi shot_partial_ms
M2b Shot-to-ready, p50 — last frame → both lanes done ≤ 40 s, warm pool, Wi-Fi shot_ready_ms
M2c Session-tail latency — last shot of a 20-shot session → ready p50 ≤ 3 min Wi-Fi, ≤ 8 min LTE session_drain_ms
M3 Analysis yield — shots reaching a state that renders numbers (ready or partial), i.e. not refused/failed ≥ 85 % Ranked, ≥ 70 % Quick shot_terminal_state
M3b Point-value yield — shots at tier A with claim_point_speed report only, no target at P0 —
M4 Coaching adherence — sessions with ≥ 3 more eligible shots after a focus opens ≥ 40 % focus_opened → session_completed.shots_after_focus_eligible
M5 Coaching efficacy — focused metric's improvement over its matched control ≥ 0.30 σ_bio, one-sided p < 0.05, ≥ 100 episodes focus_outcome.delta_vs_control
M6 Field reuse — Ranked sessions started from a saved profile ≥ 70 % by session 3 session_started.field_source = saved
M7 Band honesty — radar readings inside our reported speed_band_kmh ≥ 90 %, median band width reported alongside 03 §6.3 metric 4 / §6.5 A5
M8 Week-2 return ≥ 30 % of first-session completers session_started cohort retention
Resolved

Resolved: the latency metric. 01 §1.5 defines M2a ≤ 30 s / M2b ≤ 40 s per shot; 02 §6.4 (edit PR-1) demands M2a = first shot ≤ 75 s and M2b = last of twenty ≤ 3 min Wi-Fi / 8 min LTE; 05 Stage 2 quotes a single p50 ≤ 75 s; 03 §1.4 budgets the worker path at ≤ 40 s. These measure different spans and the old single p50 was unreachable on cellular. Kept: 01's M2a/M2b as the per-shot, warm-pool, adequate-link budgets (they compose from 04 §10.2's per-lane SLOs: 10 s upload + 20 s ball = 30 s; + 25 s pose ≈ 40 s), and 02's bandwidth arithmetic promoted to its own metric, M2c. 05's 75 s and 03's 40 s are restated as what they are: 03 §1.4's ≤ 40 s is the worker-side S1–S6 budget, and 05 Stage 2's exit criterion is M2b.

M2's derivation, warm (DERIVED from 04 §10.2's per-lane SLOs): upload ≈ 10 s + max(ball 20 s, pose 25 s). Arc-on-map ≈ 30 s (M2a, ball only); ready ≈ 35 s, rounded to 40 s (M2b) (source: 04-backend-data-apis.md §10.2). The mesh is not in the 25 s figure — S7 renders are lazy and run on tap, budget TARGET ≤ 15 s for one style against a MEASURED ~30 s for two styles / 117 s cold.

M3's definition is deliberately loose because the strict reading measures near 0 % today: BAND_ONLY is what ball.py produces on the reference clip, both shipped example fits rail a parameter, and the basin_ambiguous == false on ≥ 90 % target is future work. M3b (strict tier A) is reported with no baseline until 03 §10 gate 3 lands.

M5 uses a matched control that costs nothing. 03 §9.3 selects the focus metric because |z| ≥ 1.5 on k of the last n shots, and a metric selected for being extreme regresses toward the player's own mean regardless of coaching — so "did the focused metric move" is satisfiable by a product that does nothing. The runners-up that 03 §9.4 already computes and does not surface are the control:

control_m   = mean over runner-up flagged metrics of (x_after − x_before) / σ_bio,m   (signed toward "limiting")
delta_focus = (x_after − x_before) / σ_bio, focused metric
M5          = mean(delta_focus − control_m)  over focus episodes

σ_bio comes from the repeatability study (§8.4), which is therefore a prerequisite: until it exists M5 is not computable. A randomised hold-out arm (focus computed, not shown) is the cleaner design and is P1; the control-metric form ships first because it needs no extra users.

M7 is a gate, not a KPI. Rankings, percentiles and any point-value speed stay dark until it is measured. The single boolean that carries it is config.point_speed_released (§1.7).

1.5 Risks

# Risk L × I Mitigation
R1 Users read our number as truth, tell a friend, and are wrong by 25 % High · brand-fatal Band-not-point on every speed surface at P0; Estimated label; per-shot tier chip; enforced in the one BallFlight → ShotResultView mapper (§4.1)
R2 Licence block on the body mesh — GVHMR non-commercial, SMPL/SMPL-X research-only Certain if we ship as-is · ship-blocking Two named P0 scopes (§3.2), not a flag flip. Week-1 decision, week-2 quote + spike, hard gate at Stage 3 exit
R3 Camera moves mid-session and silently invalidates every subsequent shot High · session-fatal Continuous stability monitor; framing_lost(cause='camera_moved') blocks re-arming in Ranked; already-affected shots get invalidated_reason='camera_moved'
R4 Latency breaks the "shoot as many as you want" loop Med · core-loop Optimistic capture, analysis behind the user, on-device chips immediately, arcs at partial. Never block capture on analysis
R5 Thin cohort makes any percentile meaningless Certain at launch · trust Percentiles are Later behind the three-condition publish rule; P0 ships the personal-best → verified-benchmark → JK ladder
R6 Basin ambiguity puts a wrong arc shape on screen with full confidence Med · trust Render all surviving basins as a shape band, suppress apex/elevation
R7 GPU cost per session Low · margin TARGET ≈ $0.01/clip at target latency → a 30-shot session ≈ $0.30; measured today is the cold path. Render one hero style
R8 iPhone slo-mo share trap bakes 240 fps into a 30 fps file → silent 8× speed error (source: …/23-stryke-heroshoot/ball-accuracy-upgrade.md T1.7) High if we accept shared clips v1 accepts in-app capture only; the P1 import path carries the retime guard, and three timebase refusals already ship
R9 The field-setup ritual is too fiddly and everyone stays in Quick Mode Med-High · rankings have no data Test on a real pitch in Stage 4; completion rate per step as the primary metric; profile reuse means the cost is once per venue (M6)
R10 The P2 speed pass does not reach 8–12 s preprocess Med Explicit Stage 2 work item with four named techniques; measured over 20 real clips with per-stage timings. Fallback: S7 is already lazy and the map still fills from the ball lane
R11 JK content is a P0 dependency with a non-engineering lead time Med-High Named content owner from Stage 0 week 1; filmed at ≥ 2 registered field profiles no later than Stage 4
R12 We market an accuracy number the validation set does not support Med Standing gag order until Stage 1 publishes; every external accuracy claim needs the validation lead's sign-off; superseded tier tables marked UNVALIDATED in place

1.6 Vocabulary — fixed names for code, copy, analytics and DB columns

Nouns

Term Meaning
Session One continuous capture block at one field profile (Ranked) or one preset (Quick). Container for shots.
Shot One kick: clip window + ball flight + body motion + derived metrics.
Field profile A saved, reusable {goal dimensions, goal anchor, camera pose, shooting zone} bundle. A field profile exists only in Ranked mode; Quick sessions carry field_profile_id = NULL, which ck_ranked_needs_field makes the only legal case.
Camera placement One {camera pose, reference frame, attitude} under a field profile. A re-verify makes a new placement, not a new profile.
Shot map The 3D scene where all shots of a session accumulate as trajectories. Session-scoped; not a tab.
Focus The single highest-impact coaching item for the current period. At most one active per user.
Benchmark A comparison target: personal | jk | stryke_verified. Never "percentile" at P0.
Eligible shot A shot that may enter a coaching window: pose_confidence ≥ 0.60, c_pose == 1.0, and sharing a comparison cell with the others. Every "N shots" count in the coaching UI counts eligible shots, never raw shots.
Comparison cell Same field_profile_id, same foot, same shot_type, same 2 m distance_m bin. The coaching window key and the ranking cohort key come from the same key function in core/cohort.py.

Enums

Enum Values (code / DB) Display
Session mode quick | ranked "Quick" / "Verified"
Shot status (stored) pending_upload | uploading | uploaded | queued | analysing | partial | ready | refused | failed | invalidated —
Shot grade (derived at serve time) VERIFIED | ESTIMATED | BAND_ONLY | REFUSED "Verified" / "Estimated" pill / no pill
Confidence tier A | B | C | D chip letter + one-line meaning
Shot type free_kick | dribble | first_touch "Dead ball" / "After a dribble" / "First touch"
Zone kind free_kick_circle | dribble_zone | touch_pair —
Placement zone top_left | top_centre | top_right | mid_left | mid_centre | mid_right | low_left | low_centre | low_right | wide | over 3×3 mouth grid
Keeper tier elite | pro | club | youth "Elite Keeper" / "Pro Keeper" / … — P1 ships elite + pro only
Distance source fitted | measured | estimated estimated drives the Estimated label; only fitted ranks
Closure grade good | degraded | rejected words, never letters — see below
Resolved

Resolved: session mode is ranked, not verified. 04 §3.4 ships CheckConstraint("mode IN ('quick','ranked')") and 02 §1.2's SessionSetup already says quick | ranked. ranked is the code/DB value; Verified survives as the display name only. 03 §7.3's mode == "verified" and the AnalysisJob.mode docstring must both change. The reason it matters: a ranked-mode shot is routinely not graded VERIFIED, and collapsing the two names hides that.

Resolved: shot type is free_kick | dribble | first_touch. 01 §2.2 asserts stationary | dribble | touch and says 03 must change; 03 §9.2 and 04 §1.2 D5 both say free_kick | dribble | first_touch and 04 wins that one. 04/03's set wins, because it is what is actually shipped in three enforceable places — the ShotType StrEnum in contracts/, the ck_shot_type CHECK constraint, and the cohort_key / cell_key strings — and 01's claim that "04 §3.5 owns the column" is a claim about a column whose CHECK says free_kick. A mismatch here is a silent non-join in both the ranking cohort and the coaching cell. 01's display names are kept. The zone geometry is keyed by the same value (free_kick_circle / dribble_zone / touch_pair); it is not a second concept.

Resolved: closure_grade uses words, tier uses letters, and there is exactly one alphabet. A letter-valued calibration_tier would collide with the confidence tier that decides ranking, and with ball-trajectory.md §3's retracted accuracy tiers. Three A/B/C alphabets in one system is how a retracted accuracy claim ends up on a leaderboard.

Confidence tiers — the binding definition

There are two confidence scores, not one, because they gate different things and one formula made coaching impossible in Quick Mode:

Score Answers Gates
speed_confidence May this shot's number be ranked, and how loudly do we state it? tier, ranking eligibility
pose_confidence Is this shot's body read good enough to draw a pattern from? The coaching window
Band speed_confidence tier Additional to rank What the product may do
Verified-eligible ≥ 0.75 A mode == 'ranked' and claims.point_speed and band_width_pct ≤ 20 Ranks; feeds percentiles. Grade VERIFIED
Analysable 0.40 ≤ s < 0.75 B — Point value iff claims.point_speed and config.point_speed_released, else band; saved to history; placement and consistency may still rank
Band-only 0.20 ≤ s < 0.40 C — Band + Estimated, never a point value; not ranked
Refused s < 0.20, or any hard term zero D — "Couldn't measure this one" + reason + fix. Arc shape, curl direction and spin type still render

Bands are half-open: [0.00,0.20) D · [0.20,0.40) C · [0.40,0.75) B · [0.75,1.00] A.

Resolved

Resolved: the tier thresholds and the confidence column count. Three sets existed — 01 §2.3 (A ≥ 0.70 / B ≥ 0.55 / C ≥ 0.40 / D ≥ 0.15), 04 §3.5 (A ≥ 0.70 / B 0.40–0.70 / C 0.15–0.40 / D < 0.15, ranking gate 0.70), and 03 §7.3 (A ≥ 0.75, B 0.40–0.75, C 0.20–0.40, D < 0.20, ranking gate 0.75). 03 §7.3 wins: it owns the formula, its bands are half-open and non-overlapping, it is derived against a worked example, and 0.75 is the number 01 §2.5, 04 §9.1 and 05's definition-of-done all already quote as the ranking gate. Consequences: 04 splits shot.analysis_confidence into speed_confidence + pose_confidence, leaderboard_eligibility.confidence_threshold defaults to 0.75, and core/insights.py reads pose_confidence. With 0.75 as the A floor, tier IN ('A','B') is redundant for speed but stays meaningful for per-metric eligibility, where placement can rank on a B shot.

Resolved: does the app ever see tier D? 01 §2.2 says 02 §7.4 must add 'D'; 03 §7.3 says 02's 'A'|'B'|'C' is correct because a D shot has no number to badge. Both, in their own layer: the DB stores A|B|C|D, and the API's SpeedOut.tier is A|B|C|null — a tier-D shot serves {kind:'refused', reason} and carries no tier. No client type needs 'D'.

Status → UI state mapping (one row per UI state)

LOW_CONFIDENCE is not a status; it is a render mode derived from (status × grade).

UI chip state Stored shot.status Grade Also called
SEALED device-local, before pending_upload — 02 captured
UPLOADING uploading — —
QUEUED uploaded / queued — —
ANALYZING analysing — —
PARTIAL partial — ball lane done, pose lane still running — (the normal case; 02/05/06 must all carry it)
READY ready, tier A or B VERIFIED | ESTIMATED —
LOW_CONFIDENCE ready or partial, tier C/D, or claim_point_speed=false BAND_ONLY —
REFUSED refused REFUSED first-class success state
FAILED failed — infrastructure only
INVALIDATED invalidated (invalidated_reason='camera_moved' etc.) REFUSED —
(no chip) device-local unconfirmed / discarded — never uploaded

Device session state machine (02 §7.3, normative — "use these exact names in code and logs"):

idle → configuring → framing → armed → capturing ⇄ paused → ending → ended
                                          │
                                          ├── framing_lost ────► paused (auto)
                                          ├── thermal critical ► paused (auto)
                                          └── storage limit ───► paused (auto)

Device shot state machine — a strict superset of the server's spellings, adding only states that exist before the server has heard of the shot:

captured ─┬─► pending_upload ──► uploading ──► uploaded ──► analysing ──┬─► partial ──► ready
          │        ▲                  │                                 ├─► refused
          │        └── upload_failed ─┘  (retry)                        └─► failed
          ├─► unconfirmed ──(user keeps)──► pending_upload
          │                └──(user discards)──► discarded
          └─► invalidated        (zone_violation, multiple_balls_in_flight, user delete)

Stored session state stays created | active | completed | abandoned. 05 §1.2's settling and recalibrating are not session states: recalibrating is framing_lost with ReverifyFramingScreen open, and uploading runs concurrently with capturing.

1.7 Gates — one place, so they cannot drift

Gate Value Owner
Coaching floor 7 eligible shots in one comparison cell (n = 5 explicitly rejected) §4.6
Coaching eligibility pose_confidence ≥ 0.60 and c_pose == 1.0 and same comparison cell §4.6
Coaching significance k of last n eligible, one-sided exact binomial vs p₀ = 0.30, Holm at α/M (M = 8 candidates ⇒ 0.00625). 6-of-7 → p = 0.0038 clears; 5-of-7 → p = 0.0288 does not §4.6
Coaching cooldown The surfaced issue is the only issue for the next 3 eligible shots §4.6
Percentile publish all three: sample_users >= 30 (distinct) and sample_shots >= 200 and sample_shots_90d >= 50 §4.7
Percentile eligibility is_verified and speed_confidence >= 0.75 and tier IN ('A','B'); speed additionally needs claim_point_speed and band_width_pct <= 20 §4.7
Rankings launch Radar Run 2: 03 §6.5 A1–A13 all pass §8.5
Point speed released config.point_speed_released — one server-owned boolean, default false; flips only when A1–A5 + A11 pass §1.8
Mesh licence Signed Meshcapade licence and a written GVHMR answer, or a merged SAM-3D-Body / GEM-X migration Stage 3 exit
Resolved

Resolved: the percentile publish rule. 01 §3.9's original "n ≥ 30 shots from ≥ 10 users at confidence ≥ B" transposed the user threshold onto shots, invented a lower one, and omitted the 90-day liveness condition. 04 §9.2's three conditions win, because they are the ones the is_published flag and the nightly recompute actually implement, and the rationale is stated: thirty distinct users is the point at which one person's session cannot visibly move a decile. 06 §4.4's rung 4 must drop its extra "≥ 10 distinct users" clause.

1.8 Copy rules (product law, lint-enforceable)

The limiting-factor frame. Never "differs from the pro", "wrong", "incorrect", "you should". Individual shooting styles are legitimate.

Context Approved string
Section header on ShotDetailScreen "What may be limiting this result" / KO: "지금 결과를 제한하고 있을 수 있는 부분"
Item body "Your upper body leaned back just before impact. That may be limiting how much of your leg speed reaches the ball."
Never "Your trunk lean is wrong." · "Pros lean 6° less." · "Fix your posture."

Per-item, never composite. There is no overall posture score, form grade, or similarity percentage anywhere in the app. This retires HeroShot's 81 circular gauge by name. At P0 the right-hand column of a per-item row is a delta against a named benchmark (+3 km/h vs your best, −0.4 m vs JK) or the qualitative limiting-factor label — never a percentile, never a score out of 100.

The Estimated label sits on the number, not near it: 38–46 km/h ⟨Estimated⟩. It survives sharing as a visible watermark the server applies. It appears on ShotRailChip, ShotDetailScreen metric cards, SessionSummaryScreen, ShotShareScreen, HistoryScreen. It is not an error state and must not be styled as a warning: Quick Mode is a legitimate, encouraged mode, and an Estimated shot is a first-class record with full analysis, full history and full coaching.

Speed: band, and when a point value may ever appear.

Condition Render
P0, always kind: 'band' — 29–77 km/h. The band is the product
Later: claims.point_speed && config.point_speed_released 41 km/h + tier chip + band on tap
!claims.point_speed band, always, forever
timebase.trusted == false refused for every speed surface, the band included
Resolved

Resolved: §4.4's own internal contradiction in 01. 01 previously stated both "claims.point_speed == true → render 41 km/h" and "any point value stays dark until radar" as law. In Ranked mode with the goal in frame, a trusted timebase and ≥ 20 detections, claims.point_speed is true today — so the first rule fired at P0 on exactly the sessions the second said must stay dark. The mapper must AND in config.point_speed_released, a single server-owned boolean defaulting to false. No screen may construct kind:'value' itself.

Resolved: an untrusted time base refuses the band too. 02 §0.3 has the measurement: on a retimed clip the band [111.0, 205.8] did not cover a truth of 54.0 km/h — "only the point-value refusal saves it". So timebase.trusted == false renders refused on every speed surface. Never offer "analyse anyway with a wider band."

Session-relative superlatives are always allowed and are the P0 workhorse — "Your hardest strike today" — because correlation survives scale error (Athla Velocity: ICC 0.970 with a +6.8 % systematic bias, on tennis serves, with a metric corridor we do not have).

Coaching wording: evidence before instruction, always — "In 6 of your last 7 shots…" then "For the next three…". The evidence clause carries a count of eligible shots and is required; a card without a count is a bug. One recommendation at a time. Never coach off a single shot, and never below the 7-eligible-shot floor.

Keeper wording: "Under the Elite Keeper reaction model, this shot beats the keeper about 64 % of the time." Banned: "64 % save probability", any real player name or likeness before rights exist.

Refusal wording: name a cause and a fix, never apologise for the system — "Couldn't track this one — the ball left the frame after 6 frames. Move the phone back a couple of metres." Never use error, failed or invalid for a measurement outcome; reserve them for infrastructure.

Korean is authoritative for the launch market. English is the drafting language; the copy deck is bilingual EN/KO with a named Korean reviewer, and no string ships EN-only.

2.2 The shell — one tab bar

[ ◉ Train ]  [ Coach ]  [ Rank ]  [ JK ]  [ Me ]
  /train      /coach    /rankings  /jk    /me
Resolved

Resolved: the navigation model. Three tab sets existed — 01 §3.0 (Train · Coach · Rank · JK · Me), 02 §1.2 (TrainHome · ProgressHome · ChallengesHome · Profile, four tabs, no rankings), and 06 §5 (Train · Shots · Coach · JK · Me). 01 §3.0's five tabs win, because 01 is the only place scope is attached to a screen and because the other two each orphan a P0 destination. Shots is deleted — it resolved to no screen; ShotMapScreen is session-scoped and reached from the session summary or a history row, and HistoryScreen lives under Me. Rank is a tab so BenchmarksScreen — the P0 ladder BRIEF §7 mandates for the thin-data period — is reachable between sessions. Actions: 02 §1.2 adds coach.tsx and rankings.tsx and renames progress.tsx → MeScreen; 06 §5 replaces its list. Train is the raised centre-weighted primary tab, and CaptureScreen is entered from it — capture needs a session, so it is a button on TrainHomeScreen, not a tab.

The tab bar is hidden on /session/[sessionId]/capture and /session/[sessionId]/map, both of which are landscape-locked. Every other route is portrait-locked via expo-screen-orientation (app.json sets "orientation": "default").

2.3 Screen list

Routes are expo-router paths under apps/fe-mobile/src/app/. Scope: P0 ships for launch, P1 next, L later.

Onboarding — features/accounts

Route Screen Scope Purpose · key states
/welcome WelcomeScreen P0 Looping hero reel (accumulate → tap → mesh beat), one line of copy, Start. Bundled asset; decode failure → static poster, never a blocked button
/signin SignInScreen P0 Apple Sign-In (primary) + email OTP (Supabase Auth). States: idle · submitting · otp_sent (+ Resend in 30 s) · failed(reason) inline, never a modal · expired_link · camera_denied_readonly (lands on /me/history with a persistent bar)
/onboarding/profile ProfileSetupScreen P0 dominant_foot, height_cm, age_group (stored as birth_year), ball_size (3/4/5), units. Ball size kills a silent 7–14 % diameter-ruler bias; height is the pose pipeline's backup ruler; foot and age are cohort keys; units is the only non-measurement field. Every field has a default; Continue is never disabled
/onboarding/permissions PermissionsScreen P0 Camera + microphone + motion, each with its reason. Microphone is not decoration: audio onset is the second, independent shot-detection signal. A denied row becomes Open Settings, never a re-prompt loop
/onboarding/how-to-film HowToFilmScreen P0 Five cards, one per BLOCK-verdict framing rule (§5.5). Re-openable from /me/settings

The five cards are derived from the BLOCK rows of the framing gate — every BLOCK gets a card, WARNs do not:

# Card Blocks because
1 Turn the phone sideways Landscape is the only orientation satisfying "kicker in frame at contact" + "goal in frame" together; Capture is landscape-locked
2 Film from the side — 30–60° off the shot line Outside 20–70° is BLOCK. Down-the-axis costs a 2.4× 3D-error penalty and produces near-perfect correlation with corrupt scale
3 Phone still and braced ARKit translation < 2 cm / rotation < 0.5° over a 3 s pre-roll, else BLOCK in Ranked
4 Both posts and the crossbar in frame The goal is the only accepted metric reference
5 Close enough that the ball is big r_px < 15 is BLOCK. Standoff ceiling for r ≥ 25 px is 12.5 m at 4K, 6.3 m at 1080p

Footnote, not a card: "One ball, one player in frame." — a WARN, so it costs confidence, not the shot. Frame-rate and time-base rules are not taught: in-app capture makes them unreachable on our own captures; they surface only on the P1 import path.

Field setup — features/training

Route Screen Scope Purpose · key states
/train TrainHomeScreen P0 The launcher. Saved field profiles (name, distance, goal size, last used) — no mode badge; every saved profile is Ranked by construction — plus Quick session and Register a field. Empty → two-card chooser with the honest trade stated. Error → cached list + inline bar, never a blocking screen (local-first)
/fields/new ModePickerScreen P0 Side-by-side comparison table: records saved (both) · labelled Estimated (Quick) · counts toward rankings (Verified) · placement shown (Verified always / Quick only with the goal in frame) · setup time (0 vs ~3 min)
/session/new/quick QuickSetupScreen P0 Goal-size preset chips, a Goal will be in frame toggle, an editable auto distance estimate, a persistent Estimated preview. Calls POST /v1/training/sessions itself and routes to /session/[sessionId]/capture, skipping SessionStartScreen. Loading → — + spinner, never a placeholder number
/fields/new/verified/goal GoalDimensionsScreen P0 Preset chips + custom width_m/height_m. "Everything is measured from your goal." Goal dimensions do triple duty: metric scale, camera self-calibration by rectangle PnP, and the coordinate origin
/fields/new/verified/anchor GoalAnchorScreen P0 Live AR; walk to each of 4+ goal points and tap. States: TRACKING_LOST (capture disabled), COPLANAR_WARNING ("Add the crossbar corners — four points on a flat rectangle can't tell distance from zoom"), LOW_LIGHT. LiDAR used silently when present, never mentioned in copy
/fields/new/verified/camera CameraPlacementScreen P0 Top-down mini-map with a shaded good-camera zone, a live phone indicator and a live Angle to shot line: 72°. Tracking lost → return to anchor with anchors preserved; never silently re-anchor
/fields/new/verified/framing FramingCheckScreen P0 Live preview + overlay checklist, one row per F-check, each PASS/WARN/BLOCK. Save framing disabled while any item is red; there is no FAIL exit. Amber is allowed through and is priced into c_frame
/fields/new/verified/zone ShootingZoneScreen P0 Draggable AR ground zone + type selector (free_kick_circle / dribble_zone / touch_pair). Defaults pre-placed so the user drags rather than draws
/fields/new/verified/save FieldProfileSaveScreen P0 Name + conditions summary. Save failure → keep locally as unsynced, allow the session, retry in background. Losing a 3-minute setup to a network blip is unacceptable
/fields/[fieldId] FieldProfileScreen P0 Inspect, re-verify, rename, delete. Editing goal dimensions or distance recomputes calibration_hash; a distance edit > 1 m invalidates that field's ranked shots outright. Say so before saving
/fields/[fieldId]/reverify ReverifyFramingScreen P0 The cheap path when only the camera moved: the framing checklist with the saved anchor loaded and the previous framing ghosted at 40 %. Unrecoverable → Register as a new field, never a silent degrade

Capture session — features/training

Route Screen Scope Purpose
/session/new SessionStartScreen P0 Ranked only. Last confirmation of the conditions stamped on every shot; each row tappable to correct. Start calls POST /v1/training/sessions and fires the GPU warm-up. Create failure → start an offline session locally
/session/[sessionId]/capture CaptureScreen P0 · the core screen Landscape-locked, full-bleed preview, framing HUD, shot counter, shot rail, End session. The primary action is kicking the ball — shot detection is automatic and there is no record button in the loop
/session/[sessionId] SessionSummaryScreen P0 Shot count, mode badge, conditions strip, shot-map preview, the session's hardest strike (session-relative), any new personal best, and the coaching card only if a pattern was found across ≥ 7 eligible shots in one cell

CaptureScreen's in-session states: armed → capturing ⇄ paused, plus

  • framing_lost(cause='camera_moved') — a blocking sheet: "The camera moved. Shots from here won't be comparable." Two buttons: Re-verify framing and Continue as Estimated (downgrades the remaining shots to Quick semantics; already-sealed shots keep their grade). Never silently continue. Shots already taken after the drift get invalidated_reason='camera_moved'.
  • framing_lost(cause='no_ball' | 'out_of_zone') — capture idle, hint shown, no shot recorded.
  • paused — thermal critical or storage limit. Stop recording rather than drop frames: duplicated frames make the time base untrustworthy and assess_timebase will refuse the point value.
  • Empty — the rail shows one hint chip: "Just shoot. We'll catch it."
  • Grab last 6 s — always on screen. The ring exists anyway, so a missed detection is one tap from recovered.

ShotRailChip (component) — the user's only feedback channel during a session:

State Ring Label
SEALED grey pulse —
UPLOADING grey progress arc ↑
QUEUED grey …
ANALYZING animated arc …
PARTIAL solid brand ring + small mesh spinner the band, e.g. 38–42
READY solid brand the band, e.g. 38–42
LOW_CONFIDENCE dashed amber the band, e.g. 29–77
REFUSED dashed grey ?
FAILED dashed red !
INVALIDATED dashed grey, struck through ⊘

READY shows the band, not a midpoint — never rendering a midpoint as a measurement is product law and this is the most-seen number in the app. PARTIAL is new and is the normal case. Band widths are wide: the only measured band on a real clip is [28.8, 76.6] km/h, so every surface must lay out a two-digit-to-two-digit band up to 29–77 without truncation.

Shot map and shot detail — features/analysis

Route Screen Scope Purpose
/session/[sessionId]/map ShotMapScreen P0 · the signature screen Every shot accumulates as a faint 3D trajectory; tap one to select. Landscape; side sheet, not bottom sheet
/shots/[shotId] ShotDetailScreen P0 · the payoff screen Everything known about one shot, in an order that tells a story. Pushed portrait route
/shots/[shotId]/mesh MeshInspectorScreen P0 (scope A only) Full-screen rotatable body at impact; render runs on tap
/shots/[shotId]/compare ShotCompareScreen P0 Two shots side by side and superimposed, aligned on each clip's own kick frame; per-metric delta table
/shots/[shotId]/quality ShotQualityScreen P0 Makes our uncertainty inspectable. This screen is why users trust the rest
/shots/[shotId]/share ShotShareScreen P1 Composited clip with a server-applied Estimated watermark for Quick shots

ShotMapScreen shows, in Ranked mode: the goal drawn to the profile's real dimensions; the ground plane with the shooting-zone polygon; one faint polyline per shot at partial or ready, the selected one highlighted and thicker; goal-mouth arrival markers; placeholder rings for shots not yet partial. In Quick mode: the goal from the declared preset at the corrected distance, no zone polygon, and arrival markers only when arrival_x_m/arrival_y_m are non-null — otherwise the goal renders as an outline and the sheet shows the qualitative on_target chip.

Selection: a trajectory, its arrival marker, or its rail chip all select the same shot. Filters: This session / This field, all time / Foot / Tier ≥ B. Empty: goal and zone render alone with "Trajectories appear here as shots finish analysing." Renderer failure → the flat 2D goal-mouth placement grid, which carries most of the value with no 3D dependency and is also the Reduce-Motion-friendly view.

ShotDetailScreen shows, top to bottom:

┌────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ⟵  Shot #3        12.4 s   Right foot   ⓘ │
├────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│   REPLAY  (server-rendered mp4)   16:9     │
│   ▸ ──────●───────────  0.42s   ⟳ 0.5x    │
│                        [ Jump to impact ]  │
├────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  Speed   29 – 77 km/h   ⟨Estimated⟩  ⓘ    │   the band IS the value
│  Placement ▣ top-left   Distance 16.2 m    │
│  Confidence  ⟨B⟩ →                         │   → /shots/[shotId]/quality
├────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│   ROTATABLE 3D MESH            4:5         │   preset `mesh`
│  [ Body ] [ Skeleton ]        [ Expand ⤢ ] │   → /shots/[shotId]/mesh
├────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  BodyGuide: ▮ silhouette │ LEG SPEED       │
│                          │ 11.3 m/s        │
├────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  Speed              +2.1 km/h vs your best │   per-item, P0 form
│  Top-left placement −0.4 m vs JK           │
│  Repeat consistency Your steadiest today   │
│  Upper-body lean    may be limiting      ▸ │
├────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  ▸ One thing to change                     │   ≥ 7 eligible shots only
├────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  8-cell stat grid (tap ⇄ mesh highlight)   │
├────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  [ Compare ]                    [ Share ]  │
└────────────────────────────────────────────┘

Its states: ANALYZING (staged progress against the server's real ETA, never a fake percentage) · PARTIAL (arc, band, placement and distance all render; only the mesh card is a skeleton with "Body reconstruction still running.") · LOW_CONFIDENCE (whole screen renders, unclaimable numbers replaced by bands, top banner names the cause from quality_flags with the fix) · REFUSED (video + qualitative shape only, never a blank screen) · FAILED (retry; clip retained) · INVALIDATED (struck-through header + one line) · mesh unavailable (terminal; degrades to the 2D pose overlay on the video — the screen must be valuable without the mesh).

ShotQualityScreen shows the tier and its meaning, the detection count, which metric anchors were available — goal (goal PnP) and diameter (ball apparent diameter) — and their spread, then quality_flags in plain language, then the pipeline's own speed_refusal_reason / apex_refusal_reason strings rendered verbatim in mono, then Fix my setup → /fields/[fieldId]/reverify (Ranked) or How to film this better (Quick). There is no gravity anchor — it was removed on 2026-08-21 because solved effective g came back at 14.69 m/s², "a scale error wearing gravity's clothes". Two anchors, not three.

Coaching, JK, rankings, profile

Route Screen Scope Purpose
/coach CoachHomeScreen P0 The active focus card with its evidence, its instruction and a 0/3 ring counting eligible shots; below, Recently checked with outcomes. Empty state is a feature: "2 more shots at this distance and I can tell you what's repeating." 5 / 7, plus a row naming why shots did not count
/coach/[focusId] FocusDetailScreen P0 Evidence strip of tappable thumbnails, the drill, the linked JK lesson, and the before/after panel on the focused metric and on speed / accuracy / consistency. States active → verifying (n/3 eligible) → improved \| no_change \| inconclusive
/jk JKHomeScreen P0 Tip reel (15–30 s), 5-minute lesson grid, JK's records, Recommended for you driven by the active focus
/jk/lessons/[lessonId] JKLessonScreen P0 Video, chapters, drill, Practise this now → /train with the lesson id attached so the focus engine can attribute the change
/jk/records JKRecordsScreen P0 JK's shots as cards with conditions, speed band, placement, each with Challenge. Provenance rendered inline, never omitted
/jk/challenge/[recordId] JKChallengeScreen P0 Required conditions as a checklist matched against saved fields, JK's trajectory ghosted on the map, a live best of session vs JK strip
/rankings BenchmarksScreen P0 (ladder form only) Three-rung ladder for the selected cell — Your best · STRYKE verified benchmark · JK's record. No population, no percentile, no rank number. A rung with provenance = null is hidden entirely
/rankings/board LeaderboardScreen L Podium + table, behind the publish rule plus abuse handling
/shots/[shotId]/keeper + /keeper/result KeeperChallengeScreen, KeeperResultScreen P1 Generic tiers only (elite, pro at P1). Disabled with an explanatory chip when the shot is LOW_CONFIDENCE/REFUSED or basin_ambiguous
/me MeScreen P0 Personal bests per comparison cell, streak, total shots, the Estimated/Verified split, activity calendar, entry points
/me/history HistoryScreen P0 Sessions reverse-chronologically, filterable by field and mode
/me/profile ProfileEditScreen P0 The measurement inputs. Changing height_cm or ball_size warns it affects future analyses only; past shots keep the values they were measured with
/me/settings SettingsScreen P0 Hero style, storage + Clear local clips, data export, sign out, delete account, tour re-open. Video is PII: the delete path must delete objects, not just rows

A JK record is comparable to a user record only under a matching comparison cell — same goal dimensions, same distance bin, same shot type, same foot. Under a matching cell both measurements carry the same systematic bias and the comparison is defensible; across cells it is not. When they do not match, show the trajectory overlay (shape) but not a speed comparison. That is a server judgement (calibration_hash equality → JKRecordOut.speed_comparable), not a client computation.

Frame rate is not a user preference in Ranked mode. The app picks the format from the ladder; a Capture quality control exists for Quick mode only, and it renders the derived consequence inline: "1080p240 — keep the phone within 6.3 m of the ball." The capture screen shows the chosen mode as a live state (240 fps · 1/1000 · ISO 800, or 60 fps — speeds will be banded wider), because hiding the single largest capture-side lever from the screen that owns capture is indefensible.

03 Scope: P0 / P1 / Later

3.1 The P0 demo, honestly

BRIEF §10 asks the first-screen demo to show: shoot repeatedly → trajectories accumulate → tap one → video + 3D mesh replay → "speed top 12 %" → posture improvement point → 3D simulation vs a pro keeper → challenge JK's best. Six of the eight beats are P0. Two are not, and shipping them would require making something up.

§10 beat P0? What P0 actually shows Why
Shoot several times in a row ✅ CaptureScreen with automatic shot detection, no per-shot interaction Depends on the device detector (§4.1)
3D trajectories accumulate ✅ ShotMapScreen, faint arcs + arrival markers Arcs appear at partial, ~30 s after each strike (M2a)
Tap one ✅ Trajectory / marker / rail chip all select —
Video + 3D mesh replay ⚠️ scope-dependent Scope A: ShotDetailScreen + MeshInspectorScreen. Scope B: video + 2D pose overlay, no mesh Gated on the licence decision, not on engineering. This is not a flag flip
"speed top 12 %" ❌ → substituted "Your hardest strike today" + the band + +4 km/h vs your best at this field A percentile needs a cohort that will not exist at launch and a validated speed number that does not exist yet. Film the demo expecting a band as wide as 29–77
Posture improvement point ✅ The limiting-factor item, linked to a multi-shot focus Per-item, qualitative — but needs 7 eligible shots, so the demo session must be ≥ 7 shots in one comparison cell
3D simulation vs a pro keeper ❌ → P1 Not in the P0 build Three blockers: rights; an ambiguous input arc; and no keeper motion asset exists in any repo in the set
Challenge JK's best ✅ JKChallengeScreen under a matching cell, JK's trajectory ghosted Requires filming JK once at a registered field — content work, not research

Consequence for the launch film: shoot it against the P0 build. The keeper beat is cut or shown as a clearly-labelled concept sequence. Do not build a fake keeper simulation to make a video — that number would immediately become the thing users quote.

3.2 The two P0 scopes — mesh licence

The body mesh is P0 with a legal gate that nobody has opened, and it is the single largest scope uncertainty in this document. The build must be able to ship either shape:

Scope A — licence secured Scope B — licence not secured
ShotDetailScreen item 4 (3D mesh) Full rotatable mesh 2D pose overlay on the video
MeshInspectorScreen /shots/[shotId]/mesh Ships Not in the build — the route is absent, not an error state
ShotCompareScreen Overlap-mesh, 4-view grid Side-by-side video + 2D skeleton overlay + the per-metric delta table
KeeperResultScreen (P1) Keeper as a mesh Blocked with the mesh
Acceptance sentence (§3.4) "watches their own body in 3D at impact" "watches their own impact posture traced on the video"

Open, and D1 must answer it: no section asserts that the 2D-pose path is itself licence-clean. Scope B runs ViTPose 2D keypoints and a detector, and Ultralytics/AGPL detector weights sit on the ship-blocking path. D1 must clear the 2D stack, not only GVHMR/SMPL.

Cheap insurance while the decision is open: build the mesh viewer against the GLB artifact contract (§4.4) so the swap is worker-side, and land the joint-name-keyed PoseBackend port in week 2 so pipeline/metrics.py reads joints["r_ankle"] and never learns a rig's integer index.

3.3 Feature table

Feature Scope Rationale
Radar ground-truth set (Protocol A Run 1 + Protocol B) + published accuracy report P0 — radar ordered week 1, 3-week stage concurrent with Stage 0 Gate 0 of the CV roadmap: "Nothing else starts before this." Everything marked Later below, plus M7 and config.point_speed_released, hangs off it. ~$340 + one filming afternoon + ~2 days of analysis is the cost; 3 weeks is the duration
Onboarding: profile (foot / height / age / ball size / units) P0 Ball size and height are metric-ruler inputs, not preferences
Sign-in (Apple + email OTP, Supabase) P0 Every authenticated screen depends on it
Permissions + how-to-film gate (5 BLOCK cards) P0 Capture-side quality is the largest and cheapest accuracy lever
Quick Mode setup P0 The zero-friction path; without it the first session never happens
Ranked field registration (goal dims → AR anchor → placement → framing gate → zone → save) P0 Everything comparable in the product hangs off a saved field profile
Field reuse + re-verify framing P0 BRIEF §6: reuse the field, re-verify only the camera
Device-side shot detection + ring extraction (audio onset + luma-diff confirm) P0 engineering "Shoot as many times as you want" is the headline promise, and a record button in the loop breaks the core interaction
Server-side S1 window validator P0 engineering Re-checks the device's window, applies the three refusals server-side, trims to the exact fit window via clip_window_s
Camera-drift detection + blocking recalibration P0 Silent drift invalidates comparability, which is the whole product
Two-lane background analysis with visible states, incl. PARTIAL P0 The arc lands ~30 s after the strike and the mesh later; analysis must never block capture
P2 speed pass on the pose pipeline (preprocess 39.3 s → 8–12 s; render 30 s/2 styles → 8–15 s/1) P0 engineering M2b and the $0.01/clip figure are both stated at target latency. Measured today is the cold path; without this pass the numbers do not hold
Shot map with accumulating 3D trajectories P0 The signature screen
Shot detail: video + trajectory overlay + metrics + impact frame P0 The payoff
3D body mesh, rotatable, one hero style P0 — scope A only Scope B ships a 2D pose overlay and no MeshInspectorScreen
Speed as a band on every surface P0 Non-negotiable honesty rule; point values gated on config.point_speed_released
Confidence tier chip + ShotQualityScreen P0 Uncertainty must be inspectable or the numbers are not trustworthy
Placement — Ranked P0 Goal PnP is required for a profile to exist, so the goal frame is always present
Placement — Quick P0 when the goal is in frame and its dimensions are declared; otherwise not available Placement comes through a four-corner homography of a plane whose real dimensions the user declared — it needs no focal, no depth, no camera pose. Fallback is the qualitative on_target chip
Shot comparison: previous / personal best / JK P0 The loop that makes a 20-minute session worth it
Multi-shot coaching focus (7 eligible) + 3-eligible-shot verification P0 BRIEF §4; also M5
JK lessons, tips, records, challenge-under-matching-cell P0 The only credible benchmark at launch, and it needs no cohort
Benchmark ladder (your best → verified benchmark → JK) P0, middle rung conditional on D6 BRIEF §7's explicit instruction for the thin-data period
Session history + personal bests per comparison cell P0 Makes returning worthwhile before any social layer exists
Repeatability study → coaching candidate set P0 engineering M5 is not computable without σ_bio/σ_repeat, and any metric with σ_repeat > 0.5 σ_bio must be dropped from coaching, not caveated
Share composite with Estimated watermark P1 Growth surface; needs the render pipeline hardened first
Keeper Challenge, elite + pro, probabilistic wording P1 Rights-clean version is buildable; input-arc ambiguity must be resolved first
Import an existing clip (Quick Mode only) P1 Needs the retime/duplicate-frame guard, or an 8× silent error ships
first_touch zone type (first-touch + shooting pair) P1 free_kick and dribble zones cover the P0 demo
Randomised coaching hold-out arm P1 The cleaner M5 design; the control-metric form ships first
Percentiles among verified users Later Requires all three publish conditions and M7
Public leaderboards + podium Later Same publish rule, plus abuse handling
Point-value speed anywhere in the app Later config.point_speed_released
Named real goalkeepers; club/youth tiers Later Rights / scope
Spin magnitude as a number Never (qualitative chip only) claims.spin_magnitude is hard-wired false; Hawk-Eye's own multi-camera spin bias is −100 ± 542 RPM
Apex height / elevation in metres and degrees Later, gated on basin disambiguation Competing basins differ by up to 82 % on apex at < 2 px RMS
Android Later iOS gives exact intrinsics + ARKit metric pose; that is the accuracy path
Social feed / gallery / heroes Later HeroShot's social surfaces do not serve the training loop
Multi-player capture, match analytics Later Workstream 24, a separate product

3.4 P0 acceptance — one sentence

On a real pitch, a new user registers a field in under 3 minutes, takes 10 shots without touching the phone, sees 10 arcs on one 3D map within ~30 s of each strike, opens the fastest one, sees their own impact posture in 3D (scope A) or traced on the video (scope B), reads one thing that may be limiting the result — backed by 7 eligible shots in one comparison cell — shoots three more eligible shots to check it, and challenges JK's record at the same distance — with every speed on screen rendered as a band, and no point value anywhere.

Band width is not a P0 acceptance criterion. The band is as wide as the measurement is. The P0 requirement is that every surface lays out a 29–77-shaped band without truncation. ≤ 20 % median band width is a rankings-launch gate (A6), not a launch gate, and pretending otherwise makes P0 unshippable.

04 Technical architecture (mobile · backend · CV pipeline · 3D rendering · storage · rankings)

┌─────────────────────────── iPhone ────────────────────────────┐
│  stryke-calibrate (Swift)      stryke-capture (Swift)         │
│  ARKit ritual → T_G_from_C     AVAssetWriter · segmented ring │
│                                shot detect · framing monitor  │
│                                sidecar (per-frame K + PTS)    │
│  ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── │
│  RN/Expo:  expo-sqlite (device source of truth) · <ShotScene> │
│            <MeasuredStat> · upload drain (BGProcessingTask)   │
└──────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
               │ multipart PUT (presigned, 8 MiB parts) + REST/SSE/APNs
┌──────────────▼─────────────── be-api (FastAPI) ───────────────┐
│ accounts · fields · training · analysis · insights ·          │
│ progress · rankings · jk · challenges     +  core/ (pure)     │
│ owns EVERY analysis_job transition; mints every signed URL    │
└──────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
               │ contracts/  ← the ONLY shared surface
┌──────────────▼─────────────── be-vision (Modal) ──────────────┐
│ ball_lane  (CPU 4 vCPU/8 GB, min_containers=1)                │
│ pose_lane  (1× A10G, min_containers=0, keep_warm on session)  │
│ render_lane(1× A10G, ON TAP ONLY)                             │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
        Supabase Postgres (rows) + Supabase Storage (bytes)

Four architectural constraints govern everything below, and they come from the ADRs:

  1. Feature modules by bounded context — no domain/application/infrastructure split.
  2. contracts/ is the entire be-api ↔ be-vision surface, and be-vision never opens a database connection. New wire types go in contracts/src/stryke_contracts/vision.py and nowhere else.
  3. A new apps/ entry requires a differing dependency set. The ball lane's dependency set is stryke_vision's, so it is a second Modal entrypoint on the same image — not a new app, not in-process inside be-api (a 17 s CPU OpenCV job × 4 inside the FastAPI event loop is exactly what ADR-0001 forbids).
  4. VISION_PIPELINE=fake must keep working on a laptop with no GPU, and FakeVisionPipeline emits the full v2 result including refusal and band-only cases — those are the states the app spends most of its time in.

4.1 Mobile

Platform: iOS-first, Expo SDK 57 dev client, deployment target iOS 17.0 (driven by AVCaptureConnection.videoRotationAngle). apps/fe-mobile/AGENTS.md is binding: "Expo HAS CHANGED. Read the exact versioned docs at https://docs.expo.dev/versions/v57.0.0/ before writing any code."

apps/fe-mobile/
├── app.json                       # the ONLY place Info.plist / capabilities are configured
├── modules/                       # Expo local modules (Swift), autolinked by prebuild
│   ├── stryke-capture/ios/
│   │   ├── CaptureSession.swift   # AVCaptureSession config
│   │   ├── SegmentRing.swift      # rolling segmented writer + sample-passthrough cut
│   │   ├── ShotDetector.swift     # audio onset + luma-diff confirm + zone test
│   │   ├── FramingMonitor.swift   # IMU + visual drift + thermal
│   │   ├── ThumbnailRing.swift    # 10 Hz 480×270 BGRA ring for the shot rail
│   │   ├── Sidecar.swift          # writes shot_<n>.json
│   │   └── Uploader.swift         # background URLSession, presigned parts
│   └── stryke-calibrate/ios/      # ARKit ritual: ground plane, camera pose, LiDAR assist
└── src/
    ├── app/                       # expo-router routes — thin
    ├── features/{training,analysis,progress,challenges,accounts}/   # mirrors be-api contexts
    ├── db/                        # expo-sqlite schema + DAOs — device source of truth
    ├── render/                    # <ShotScene> contract + renderSpec.generated.ts
    ├── api/                       # client.ts + GENERATED schema.d.ts (never hand-edited)
    ├── components/  └── hooks/
Resolved

Resolved: the generated render-spec path. There is one 3D concern, so there is one directory: src/render/renderSpec.generated.ts. 06 §2.2's src/three/… changes to match — src/three/ would be a lie if the renderer gate lands on anything other than three.js.

Camera: a Swift AVFoundation Expo module (stryke-capture), not VisionCamera. Four concrete reasons, each tied to a measured requirement:

Requirement Why VisionCamera does not carry it
Per-frame camera intrinsic matrix isCameraIntrinsicMatrixDeliveryEnabled must be set before startRunning(), is supported only on AVCaptureVideoDataOutput (so the file must be written by AVAssetWriter), and is unsupported with cinematic stabilization. This deletes the ±15 % diagonal-focal heuristic, which is worth a 2.4× span in solved speed on one real clip (source: …/23-stryke-heroshoot/ball-accuracy-upgrade.md T3.1; …/out/goal_anchored/scene_measured.json)
A retroactive ring buffer Auto-segmenting a session requires cutting a window out of footage already recorded when the shot was detected. VisionCamera has startRecording/stopRecording only — without a ring, detection would have to be predictive
An honest time base duplicate_frame_ratio ≥ 0.50 or a container-rate disagreement makes timebase.trusted false. Owning the AVAssetWriter means we own the PTS
Worklets collision VisionCamera frame processors need react-native-worklets-core; this app ships react-native-worklets@0.10.1 (the Reanimated 4 runtime). Two worklet runtimes in one binary buys nothing, because our frame work happens in Swift on the capture queue

This argument cuts both ways: if the chosen 3D renderer pulls react-native-worklets-core in, either the VisionCamera rejection is void or that renderer is. Both are checked in the same afternoon (§10, Stage 0 spikes).

The load-bearing capture configuration, with the ordering rule stated, because adding an output and committing a configuration can reset the device's active frame duration:

// lock → set format → unlock → add outputs → configure connection → commit → lock again → pin
let device = AVCaptureDevice.default(.builtInWideAngleCamera, for: .video, position: .back)!
// NOT .builtInTripleCamera: a virtual device switches physical lenses mid-recording,
// which silently changes K in the middle of a flight.
guard let rank = pickFormat(device, ladder: config.ladder, light: meteredLight) else {
    throw CaptureError.noSupportedFormat
}
session.beginConfiguration()
session.sessionPreset = .inputPriority          // the format decides, not a preset
try device.lockForConfiguration(); device.activeFormat = rank.format; device.unlockForConfiguration()

let video = AVCaptureVideoDataOutput()
video.alwaysDiscardsLateVideoFrames = false     // we want every frame, or a counted drop
video.setSampleBufferDelegate(self, queue: captureQueue)
guard session.canAddOutput(video) else { throw CaptureError.cannotAddVideoOutput }
session.addOutput(video)
let audio = AVCaptureAudioDataOutput(); audio.setSampleBufferDelegate(self, queue: audioQueue)
if session.canAddOutput(audio) { session.addOutput(audio) }

guard let conn = video.connection(with: .video) else { throw CaptureError.noConnection }
if conn.isVideoStabilizationSupported { conn.preferredVideoStabilizationMode = .off }
if conn.isVideoRotationAngleSupported(0) { conn.videoRotationAngle = 0 }        // iOS 17+
guard conn.isCameraIntrinsicMatrixDeliverySupported else {                       // the gate
    throw CaptureError.noIntrinsics(rank.label)   // -> fall back one ladder rank, badge the session
}
conn.isCameraIntrinsicMatrixDeliveryEnabled = true      // MUST precede startRunning()
session.commitConfiguration()

try device.lockForConfiguration()
device.activeVideoMinFrameDuration = rank.frameDuration
device.activeVideoMaxFrameDuration = rank.frameDuration          // min == max => CFR, no VFR
let dur = clamp(rank.targetExposure, rank.format.minExposureDuration, rank.format.maxExposureDuration)
let iso = clamp(meteredISO, rank.format.minISO, rank.format.maxISO)
if device.isExposureModeSupported(.custom) { device.setExposureModeCustom(duration: dur, iso: iso, completionHandler: nil) }
if device.isFocusModeSupported(.locked)    { device.setFocusModeLockedWithLensPosition(framingLensPosition, completionHandler: nil) }
if device.isWhiteBalanceModeSupported(.locked) { device.whiteBalanceMode = .locked }
device.videoZoomFactor = 1.0                  // any zoom invalidates the intrinsics record
device.unlockForConfiguration()
session.startRunning()

Format ladder — ordered by pixels on the ball first, shutter second, frame rate third, because v_metric = v_image_plane × Z / f: in-plane speed contributes < 1 % error while the depth ruler contributes 10–28 % and transfers 1:1, and the ball's apparent diameter is the depth ruler (source: …/23-stryke-heroshoot/ball-accuracy-upgrade.md §2, error-term propagation table). Motion blur biases that ruler — an inflated apparent diameter under-estimates Z and under-states speed — so a long exposure is not a neutral trade for light.

Rank Format Shutter Streak (fraction of ball Ø) Standoff for r ≥ 25 px Bytes/s (ASSUMED) When
1 3840×2160 @ 120 fps HEVC 1/1000 s, ISO ≤ 1600 0.13 D 12.5 m ~15 MB/s Bright daylight, iPhone 15 Pro and newer only
2 3840×2160 @ 60 fps HEVC 1/1000 s, ISO ≤ 1600 0.13 D 12.5 m ~7.5 MB/s The default. Every device at the iOS 17 floor
3 3840×2160 @ 60 fps HEVC 1/500 s, ISO ≤ 2000 0.25 D 12.5 m ~7.5 MB/s Overcast
4 1920×1080 @ 240 fps HEVC 1/1000 s 0.13 D 6.3 m — the HUD hard-caps standoff ~7.5 MB/s 4K unavailable, or genuinely inside 6 m (indoor, futsal)
5 1920×1080 @ 120 fps HEVC 1/500 s 0.25 D 6.3 m ~3.8 MB/s Rank 4 unavailable or ISO would exceed 2000
6 1920×1080 @ 60 fps HEVC 1/240 s, blur_suspected in the sidecar 0.52 D 6.3 m ~1.9 MB/s Low light. 60 fps is what unlocks a long exposure; the streak is flagged so the server can de-bias it
— anything < 24 fps effective — — — — Refuse
Resolved

Resolved: the format ladder's rank order. 02 §2.3 ranks 4K first (4K120 opportunistic, 4K60 default, 1080p240 at rank 4); 03 §5's F1 table labels rank 1 as 1080p240 and rank 4 as 4K60 while declaring 02's ladder normative; 01 §3.10 says "prefer 4K120 over 1080p240". 02 §2.3's ladder above is normative — it is the version derived from the error propagation, and 03 is quoting a stale copy of the table it defers to. The geometry argument is about resolution, not frame rate: Z_max = 0.0044 · f_px, so 4K60 delivers the same 12.5 m standoff on every device at the iOS 17 floor, at half rank 1's bytes. The device matrix survives intact and rank 1 is an opportunistic bonus. Actions: 03 §5's F1-in-practice table relabels its rungs; 01 §3.10's Ranked-mode line becomes "the app picks 4K60, or 4K120 where the device, the light and the thermal state allow".

Rank availability is a per-device probe, not a table — AVCaptureDevice.formats is the authority and the ladder is a preference order over what it reports. The bytes/s column is ASSUMED and every downstream figure hangs off it; the mobile build's step 2 measures it once and the measured numbers replace these.

Photos-library import is Quick Mode only (P1). An imported clip sets intrinsics.source: "diagonal_heuristic" and, if the timebase probe fails, timebase.trusted: false — at which point the app renders REFUSED for every speed surface, the band included. Imported clips never enter a ranked cohort.

Shot detection on device — audio onset triggers, luma-diff departure confirms:

band-pass 500 Hz – 8 kHz  →  short-term energy E[n]        (48 kHz, 512-sample hops = 10.7 ms)
onset  ⇔  E[n] / median(E[n-46 … n-1]) > 6.0   AND   E[n] > floor_dbfs
refractory: 800 ms

resting ROI = the ball's last confident at-rest box (seeded at arm time, tracked between shots)
departure   ⇔ ROI mean |Δluma| > θ_d for 2 consecutive frames
            AND a fast-moving blob appears within 6 ROI-widths, moving > 3 px/frame

The luma proxy is a strided gather from the Y plane (stride 20 at 1080p, 40 at 4K) into a 96×54 buffer — a few thousand byte loads, not a vImageScale over an 8.3 Mpx plane. The per-frame budget is 4.17 ms at 240 fps / 16.7 ms at 4K60 (DERIVED: 1/fps); nothing establishes that the work fits until it is measured, and the build gate measures it (p99 ≤ 3 ms/frame, 0 dropped frames over 8 minutes).

Fusion, with the speed-of-sound correction that makes a ±3-frame tolerance physically reachable. Sound travels 343 m/s, so at a 10 m standoff the audio onset arrives 29 ms — 7 frames at 240 fps — after the visual departure. The calibration already knows the distance, so correcting is free:

t_audio_corrected = t_audio − (shooterDistanceM / 343.0) − audioHardwareLatencyS
Δ                 = t_audio_corrected − t_visual_departure
Audio Vision Δ Verdict What happens
✓ ✓ |Δ| ≤ 3 frames confirmed Cut clip, trigger.confidence = 0.9, queue for upload
✓ ✓ 3 frames < |Δ| ≤ 400 ms confirmed_loose Cut clip, trigger.confidence = 0.6, S1 re-checks server-side
✓ ✗ — unconfirmed Cut anyway, badge amber in the tray, do not auto-upload
✗ ✓ — confirmed_visual Cut clip, trigger.source: "vision", confidence 0.7
✗ ✗ — missed Grab last 6 s

The segmented ring — two AVAssetWriter instances alternating 8-second HEVC segments in Library/Caches/stryke/ring/, both with AVVideoMaxKeyFrameIntervalDurationKey: 0.5 so every point is within 0.5 s of a preceding IDR:

window = [T − 1.0 s, T + 2.5 s]                          // 3.5 s, spans at most 2 segments
cut    = nearest preceding IDR at or before T − 1.0 s    // <= 0.5 s earlier
→ AVAssetReader (outputSettings: nil) → AVAssetWriter (outputSettings: nil, sourceFormatHint)
   i.e. append the compressed CMSampleBuffers unchanged — NO decode, NO re-encode
→ Library/Application Support/stryke/sessions/<sessionId>/shot_<n>.mov  +  shot_<n>.json

Three properties that must be stated correctly: AVAssetExportSession cannot do this (a window spanning two segment files needs a composition whose splice is only passthrough-able at an IDR; sample-level passthrough has no splice problem at all); the exported window is not exactly [T−1.0, T+2.5] (it starts at the nearest preceding IDR, and the sidecar records trim_lead_s so AnalysisJob.clip_window_s carries the server-side trim); and the claim is "no re-encode after the ring's own encode", not "bit-identical to what the sensor produced" — the ring already lossy-HEVC encoded them. Pre-roll is 1.0 s (the tracker builds a background median and localises the resting ball from pre-departure frames); the tail is a fixed 2.5 s because goal crossing is a fit output the device cannot know in real time.

Consequences: a 300 ms-late trigger loses nothing — detection has to be reliable, not fast; the temporal crop is free and happens before any upload; and we never keep a whole session tape (at rank 2 an 8-minute tape is ~3.6 GB; the ring is ~120 MB and each shot lands at ~26 MB).

The device also owns the three S1 refusals at capture time: a window whose ball never departs (unconfirmed, held in the tray); a window where the ball leaves from outside the shooting zone (the profile's shot_zone polygon reprojected to image space once at arm time using the frozen T_G_from_C, then point-in-polygon tested → zone_violation, kept, badged, never ranked; Quick has no zone so zone_checked: false); and overlapping windows (two departure events with non-overlapping ROIs inside one 3.5 s window → multiple_balls_in_flight, both badged, neither auto-uploads).

Shot-rail thumbnails come from a separate ThumbnailRing: 480×270 BGRA at 10 Hz, 2.0 s deep = 20 frames ≈ 10.4 MB resident (DERIVED), picked nearest the visual departure and JPEG-encoded off the capture queue. Retaining full-res CVPixelBuffers for 1.0 s at 240 fps would be ~2 GB. (Rail-chip promise: 01-product.md §3.3.)

Thermals are a first-class channel, because iOS throttles by dropping frames, which is exactly the duplicate_frame_ratio condition ball.py refuses on (source: ~/Desktop/programming/stryke-pipeline/docs/BALL.md timebase block):

ProcessInfo.thermalState Session FSM UI
nominal — nothing
fair log to the sidecar thermal chip in the HUD
serious drop one ladder rank if a lower-bitrate rank exists non-blocking banner offering End session
critical → paused, recording stopped blocking sheet: "Your phone is too hot to keep filming without dropping frames."

thermal_state_at_capture is a column on shots and a field in the sidecar, so a session that degraded is diagnosable rather than mysterious.

State libraries — deliberately small: expo-sqlite (wrapped in DAOs) is the source of truth for anything the user captured; reactive reads go through useSyncExternalStore over a shotStore; the in-progress session lives natively in stryke-capture with JS subscribing via an EventEmitter (the capture session survives JS reloads and is authoritative about the AVCaptureSession); and @tanstack/react-query covers server-only reads (benchmarks, cohorts, JK records).

The type that prevents the app from lying. There is exactly one component that renders a Measured<number>, and no screen may format a raw float into a stat:

// src/features/analysis/measured.ts
import type { components } from '@/api/schema';           // GENERATED. Never hand-edited.
type Tier = 'A' | 'B' | 'C';                              // a D-tier shot is `refused`, no tier

export type Measured<T> =
  | { kind: 'value';   value: T; band: [T, T]; tier: Tier }
  | { kind: 'band';    band: [T, T]; reason: string; tier: Tier | null }
  | { kind: 'refused'; reason: string };

export class ContractViolation extends Error {}

export function speedFrom(
  s: components['schemas']['SpeedOut'],
  timebaseTrusted: boolean,
  pointSpeedReleased: boolean,          // config.point_speed_released — server-owned
): Measured<number> {
  if (!timebaseTrusted) return { kind: 'refused', reason: 'timebase_untrusted' };
  if (!s.claimed || !pointSpeedReleased) {
    if (!s.band_kmh) return { kind: 'refused', reason: s.reason ?? 'no_measurement' };
    return { kind: 'band', band: s.band_kmh, reason: s.reason ?? 'unclaimed', tier: s.tier ?? null };
  }
  if (s.kmh == null) throw new ContractViolation('claimed=true with kmh=null');
  return { kind: 'value', value: s.kmh, band: s.band_kmh, tier: s.tier as Tier };
}

export interface ShotResultView {
  speedKmh: Measured<number>;
  apexM: Measured<number>;          // TrajectoryOut.apex_m + apex_reason
  elevationDeg: Measured<number>;
  distanceM: Measured<number>;
  placement: { xM: number; yM: number; zone: string; onTarget: boolean } | null;  // never (0,0)
  spin: { type: 'topspin'|'backspin'|'flat'; curl: 'left'|'right'|'none';
          confidence: 'low'|'medium'|'high' } | null;     // qualitative ONLY, never a rate
  trajectory: { frame: 'G' | 'B'; points: Vec3[] } | null;
  basins: components['schemas']['BasinSolution'][];       // render ALL when basin_ambiguous
  qualityFlags: string[];                                 // → recording-coach tips
}

Three CI gates enforce it: ShotResultView cannot be constructed from a payload lacking claims (type-level never on the un-narrowed branch plus a runtime ContractViolation); the v1 ShotObservation.speed_mps field is not importable from any file under src/features/ (eslint no-restricted-imports, because the v1 type survives for one release and is exactly what would get grabbed by mistake); and a snapshot test over FakeVisionPipeline's distribution (70 % band-only, 15 % point value, 10 % refused, 5 % failed) asserts that no rendered screen contains a number in the refused and band-only cases.

The recording coach maps pipeline quality flags to next-session actions — the loop that upgrades a user's capture quality and therefore their accuracy:

Flag / condition What the app says Where
metric_reference: false "Get the goal in frame — that's the difference between a range and a number." Shot detail, above the band
timebase.trusted: false / retimed_suspected "This clip was re-shared or edited. Film in STRYKE for a real speed." Shot detail, replacing every speed surface; blocks ranked entry
n_detections < 20 "The ball got too small or too blurry. Move 1–2 m closer." Shot detail
azimuth_deg < 30 "Step round to the side — filming down the shot line costs the most accuracy of anything you can change." Framing HUD, live
azimuth_deg > 60 "Step back toward the shot line a little so the goal isn't square-on to you." Framing HUD, live
maxBackgroundShiftPx > 3 "The phone moved. Brace it or use a tripod." End of session
ISO > 2000 "Low light is costing you sharpness. Try earlier in the day." End of session
blur_suspected (rank 6) "It was dark enough that we had to leave the shutter open — the ball will look streaked and the speed is a range." End of session
thermal_state_at_capture ≥ serious "Your phone got hot and started dropping frames. Shade it, or shoot in shorter sets." End of session

Sign convention, stated once: azimuth_deg is the angle off the shot line with 90 = side-on and 0 = down the axis, everywhere — HUD, sidecar, server, copy.

Native modules, with their build implications (this table's whole purpose is "what needs a new build"):

Module What it is app.json → ios keys OTA?
stryke-capture (local, Swift) Capture, ring, detection, framing + thermal monitor, thumbnails, sidecar, uploader NSCameraUsageDescription, NSMicrophoneUsageDescription, NSMotionUsageDescription; UIBackgroundModes: ["processing","remote-notification"]; BGTaskSchedulerPermittedIdentifiers: ["com.stryke.app.upload-drain"] ❌ — so all tunables (onset ratio, refractory, drift thresholds, ring length, ladder) are JS-supplied at configure() time, never Swift constants
stryke-calibrate (local, Swift) ARKit ritual adds arkit to UIRequiredDeviceCapabilities ❌
3D renderer (undecided, §4.4) — A/C: none · B: a pod A/C: shaders + scene are JS ⇒ ✅ · B: ❌

expo-file-system Files; possibly the uploader — ✅
expo-background-task + expo-task-manager Registers the BGProcessingTask — expo-file-system does not UIBackgroundModes: ["processing"] ❌ registration, ✅ handler

expo-notifications APNs silent push when the pose lane settles remote-notification, an APNs key, aps-environment ❌ entitlement, ✅ handling
expo-image-picker Photos import, Quick only (P1) NSPhotoLibraryUsageDescription ❌ usage string, ✅ flow
@shopify/react-native-skia Framing HUD, goal reticle, 2D overlays — ✅ · ~5 MB, Fabric-ready
expo-screen-orientation Landscape lock on capture + map requires "orientation": "default" ✅
expo-haptics Contact-frame detent, selection feedback — ✅ · banned while recording: the phone is on a tripod, haptics shake it, and the taptic click pollutes the audio-onset detector
expo-secure-store Tokens (Keychain), never AsyncStorage — already in the scaffold
❌ expo-sensors not used — stryke-capture owns CMMotionManager directly — the IMU stream must be timestamp-aligned with video frames, which a JS-bridged sensor API cannot do

Device matrix. iPhone 12 is the performance baseline and every gate number is measured on it.

Device Chip Rank 1 (4K120) Rank 2 (4K60) Rank 4 (1080p240) LiDAR Status
iPhone 17 / 17 Pro A19 / A19 Pro ✅ ✅ ✅ Pro only Fully supported
iPhone 16 / 16 Pro A18 / A18 Pro ✅ ✅ ✅ Pro only Fully supported
iPhone 15 Pro A17 Pro ✅ ✅ ✅ ✅ Fully supported
iPhone 15 A16 ❌ ✅ ✅ ❌ Fully supported at rank 2
