Resolution: 05 row 15 is restated as "reaches VERIFIED like a LiDAR one, conditional on task C-2", and two fallbacks are pre-approved — promote the tap-based closure (four corner taps + pinned intrinsics → f_px · 7.32 / w_px) to the primary width measurement and demote the walk to a cross-check (the tap path does not accumulate VIO drift), or gate per device class with separate width_err_pct percentiles. A10 reports the two classes separately either way.

5.4 Quick mode calibration

No walk, no field profile, no ARKit ritual. Film immediately. The automatic distance estimate takes the best available:

  1. Goal visible + dimensions declared → the goal anchor as above. Honest error on shot_distance_m: ±15 %, dominated by the focal heuristic.
  2. Ball diameter at contact: Z = f_px · R_ball / r_px, R_ball = 0.11 m for size 5 (size 4 = 0.1025). Honest error: 24–28 % relative, before the focal error is added.
  3. Nothing → the module refuses a point value and emits the band only.

Why Quick-mode distance is ±15 % and not ±3 %. Z = f_px · W / w_px, and a ±15 % focal error transfers to Z 1:1 — the same propagation, the same line of algebra, as it does to speed. shot_distance_m is a metric length in the goal frame, so it needs the same metric scale and there is no focal-free path to it. (A four-corner homography is focal-free, but only for points on the goal plane, and the launch spot is 10–35 m in front of it.) Any "±3 % distance" figure is the goal anchor's relative term quoted without the focal term that dwarfs it.

The genuinely focal-free Quick-mode result, and the thing to build the Quick screen around: the ball's arrival position in goal-mouth coordinates (X_G, Y_G) comes straight through the four-corner homography of a plane whose real dimensions the user declared. It needs no focal, no depth, no camera pose. So in Quick mode placement is a real measurement while speed and distance are estimates — which is exactly why eligible_metrics is per-metric rather than all-or-nothing.

Quick configuration Honest shot_distance_m Placement Honest speed Shown as Tier
Goal in frame, dims declared, oblique ±15 % (focal-dominated) focal-free, ~±0.1 m point value only if timebase trusted and ≥ 20 detections; else band number or band + Estimated B
Goal in frame, fronto-parallel ±15 % focal-free, ~±0.1 m band only (focal unresolved by PnP) band + Estimated B
No goal, ball radius ≥ threshold, ≥ 20 detections ±25–35 % not available band only band + Estimated C
No goal, 8–19 detections ±25–35 % not available band only (only_N_detections_speed_is_band_only) band + Estimated C
< 8 detections, no departing track, or r_px < 15 at contact refuse refuse refuse "Couldn't measure this one — film from the side, closer" D
Resolved

Resolved: the 8–19 detection band. An earlier table refused everything below 20 detections. ball.py raises only below 8; 8 ≤ N < 20 emits only_{N}_detections_speed_is_band_only and is band-only, not refused — which is also the only reading under which c_det's ramp over that range means anything.

Every Quick result carries mode: "quick", verified_eligible: false, and is excluded from percentiles and rankings — only from those. A Quick shot gets full analysis, full history, and full coaching.

5.5 The framing gate

The gate the app runs before it will let a session be Ranked. Three verdicts: BLOCK (cannot start a Ranked session), WARN (start, but c_frame is penalised), PASS.

# Check Threshold If failed Source
F1 Ball apparent radius at contact r_px ≥ 25 → standoff Z ≤ 0.0044 · f_px < 25 px WARN; < 15 px BLOCK 15 px is the measured degradation floor ("below ~15 px detections degraded to noise and the track was cut"); ball.py's …below_12… is the looser shipped backstop
F2 Both posts + crossbar in frame all 4 corners inside with ≥ 24 px margin BLOCK in Ranked the goal is the only accepted metric reference
F3 Goal is not fronto-parallel post-length asymmetry ≥ 4 % or crossbar angle ≥ 2.5° WARN (scale-only anchor; focal unresolved) detect_goal's degeneracy test
F4 Camera off the shot axis azimuth_off_axis_deg ≥ 30° 20–30° WARN; < 20° BLOCK. Side-on ~90° PASSES see below
F5 Camera still framing_ok sustained over a 3 s pre-roll framing_drifted WARN; framing_lost BLOCK in Ranked §5.6's table
F6 Effective frame rate fps × (1 − duplicate_ratio) ≥ 24 BLOCK effective_fps_{x}_below_24
F7 Time base trustworthy duplicate_frame_ratio < 0.5, no VFR, no container-rate disagreement BLOCK in Ranked (in-app capture should make this unreachable) assess_timebase
F8 Exactly one ball ≤ 1 persistent white circular candidate in the 2 s pre-roll besides the match ball WARN a hanging spare ball was a persistent false-lock source for all three prototype trackers
F9 Camera elevated height_m ≥ 1.2 and solved tilt ≤ 45° WARN non-coplanar PnP wants elevation
F10 Whole arc stays in frame predicted arc from the last 3 shots of this profile stays ≥ 40 px inside the edges WARN see below
F11 Contrast ball-vs-background luma contrast ≥ 25/255 in the contact ROI WARN —
Resolved

Resolved: F4 permits side-on. An earlier draft blocked side-on ~90° and cited TT3D as the reason, which reads the measurement backwards. TT3D's numbers under identical noise are side 12.4 cm / oblique 17.1 cm / back 29.8 cm (source: …/23-stryke-heroshoot/ball-accuracy-upgrade.md T3.3): side-on is the best condition, oblique is 38 % worse, and the 2.4× penalty belongs to filming down the axis. The source's own recommendation is titled "Make side-on the supported mode; demote down-the-axis to band-only." F4 blocks only down-the-axis. Losing the goal at 90° is F2's job and losing the metric anchor is F3's — encoding either in F4 blocked the best condition twice over. The 30–60° band stays as the coached target, because that is …/23-stryke-heroshoot/ball-trajectory.md S2 verbatim ("Camera 30–60° off the kick direction… Don't go full 90° if you want the goal in frame for homography") and because it is the band that is side-on enough for most of the speed to live in the image plane and oblique enough that the goal rectangle is not fronto-parallel, which is what non-coplanar PnP needs to separate focal from depth. Sequencing matters more than the gate: until task S4-1 widens the az bound, a side-on clip rails and is refused for a solver-box reason, so Run 1 must be filmed after S4-1 or with the bound temporarily widened and the change recorded in the run README.

Camera off-axis Ball-speed geometry Goal in frame for the metric anchor
~15° (down-the-axis) worst — 2.4× 3D error, speed lives in depth usually yes
30–60° (oblique) good — 17.1 cm yes, and non-coplanar, which also solves focal
~90° (side-on) best — 12.4 cm usually no; the goal leaves the frame or goes edge-on

F10's justification is arc length, not gravity. An earlier draft justified it with the gravity ruler's window sensitivity — but that ruler was deleted, and justifying a live gate with the error behaviour of a removed anchor is exactly the stale reasoning this design is meant to catch. What still argues for framing the whole arc: more flight in frame is more real detections (which is c_det and the ≥ 20 threshold for a point value at all); the competing basins agree on reprojection but disagree about where the ball comes down, so a truncated arc removes the evidence that separates them and basin_ambiguous stays true; and the truncation pass needs something to truncate.

F1 in practice (DERIVED): r_px = f_px · 0.11 / Z, so Z_max = 0.0044 · f_px.

Ladder rank Capture mode f_px (≈68° HFOV) Max standoff for r ≥ 25 px
2 (default) 3840×2160 @ 60 fps 2846 12.5 m
4 1920×1080 @ 240 fps 1423 6.3 m

Frame rate does not buy in-plane speed precision — the propagation gives SE 0.48 km/h, 0.5 %, at 30 fps with N = 11 and σ = 10 px (source: …/23-stryke-heroshoot/ball-accuracy-upgrade.md §2); it buys sharper balls and a cleaner contact frame. Pixels on the ball buy the depth ruler. The framing overlay is a live rectangle plus a shot-axis wedge, not a typed distance — no consumer app in the survey asks the user for a measured number.

The HUD is the app's implementation of this gate, and its verdict vocabulary is the gate's:

HUD element Gate Threshold Verdict
Goal quad reticle — the profile's corners reprojected live F2 4 corners inside with ≥ 24 px margin BLOCK in Ranked
Ball-size ring — target apparent radius at the contact distance F1 r_px ≥ 25; < 15 px blocks WARN / BLOCK
Side-on wedge — camera heading vs the goal-plane normal F4 ∈ [30°, 60°]; WARN 20–30 or 60–70; BLOCK outside 20–70 —
Stillness bar F5 §5.6 BLOCK in Ranked
Shutter/ISO chip — 240 fps · 1/1000 · ISO 800 F11, blur ISO > 2000 → WARN WARN
Thermal chip — appears only at fair or worse — serious → banner, critical → paused —

5.6 Camera movement, and the re-verify flow

The ARKit → AVFoundation handoff. stryke-calibrate runs a plain ARSession (ARWorldTrackingConfiguration) — not ARKit + RealityKit rendering; we want pose and plane only, and the reticle is drawn in Skia over the preview. ARKit caps at ~60 fps, so it is a ritual, not the capture (source: …/23-stryke-heroshoot/ball-accuracy-upgrade.md T3.2). An ARSession and a custom AVCaptureSession cannot own the camera simultaneously, so ARKit's world frame is destroyed at the switch and "with the phone unmoved" carries the entire calibration:

1. ARSession running, ritual complete, T_G_from_C recorded.
2. Start CMDeviceMotion (50 Hz) BEFORE pausing the ARSession. Record attitude q0.
3. ARSession.pause() → configure AVCaptureSession → startRunning()
4. Record attitude q1 from the same CMMotionManager stream.
5. If angle(q0, q1) > 0.3°  →  REJECT the placement, return to the ritual.
   Otherwise T_G_from_C is carried forward and frozen for the session.

CMDeviceMotion runs across the switch because it is owned by neither session, which is what makes the check possible at all. 0.3° is ASSUMED; the build gate measures the distribution over 20 handoffs and the cut moves to its 90th percentile.

K does not come from the ritual. ARFrame.camera.intrinsics describes ARKit's own capture format — a different resolution and sensor crop — so fx, fy, cx, cy all change with format and an ARKit K does not transfer to 4K60 or 1080p240. It is also not needed: the whole argument for building stryke-capture is that AVFoundation delivers the exact per-frame K for the format actually recording. The ritual supplies pose; AVFoundation supplies K. ARKit's K is recorded as arkit_ritual_fx purely as a cross-check, and a disagreement > 2 % means one of the two is reading a different lens and the registration is rejected.

Resolved

Resolved: there is no ARKit during capture. 03 §3.5 step 3 says "During capture, watch ARFrame.camera.transform: translation > 2 cm or rotation > 0.5°", and S1's input list names an "(Verified) ARKit camera pose stream". Neither exists under this design, and cannot. Both are replaced by FramingMonitor's two channels below.

FramingMonitor — three independent channels, because they fail on different axes. Thermal is covered in §4.1; the other two:

  • IMU — CMMotionManager.deviceMotion at 50 Hz, .xArbitraryZVertical: attitudeDriftDeg = angle between the current attitude quaternion and the handoff q1; shockDetected = |userAcceleration| > 0.35 g. Note what the IMU cannot do: translation. Double-integrating accelerometer output drifts metres within seconds, so "the camera moved 2 cm" is not directly observable once ARKit is off — it is observable indirectly, as background pixel shift.
  • Visual — a vImageScale_Planar8 of the Y plane to 960×540, every 12th frame (20 Hz at 240 fps, 5 Hz at 4K60): sparse Lucas–Kanade on ~120 Shi-Tomasi corners against a referenceFrameGray stored at the same 960×540, RANSAC affine, reporting the median background displacement in full-resolution pixels with the scale factor written out.
Resolved

Resolved: the camera-movement thresholds. Three sets existed: 03 §3.5 / 05 rows 1–2 (ok < 0.5° and < 8 px; drifted 0.5–2.0° or 8–40 px; lost > 2.0°, > 40 px, > 0.08 m, or shock) and 02 §4.2's derived set. 02 §4.2's derived set wins, and the 40 px ceiling is deleted. The 40 px was justified as "half of the prototype's ~80 px pan", but the source does not state the resolution that 80 px was measured at, so the halving was not a derivation — and 40 px is 13× the CV section's own 3 px fallback. Converting the intent (2 cm of translation, 0.5° of rotation) to the observable at a 10 m standoff, f = 1423 px at 1080p: 2 cm ⇒ 2.8 px (which is the 3 px fallback, from the other direction — the two agree) and 0.5° ⇒ 12.4 px. The buffer resolution also had to move: at 96×54 against a 1080p source the scale factor is 20×, so an "8 px" boundary was 0.4 px on the buffer — sub-pixel LK on a 5,184-pixel frame that does not contain 60 distinguishable corners. It was not a measurement.

State Condition (full-res px; 960×540 equivalent in brackets) Effect
framing_ok attitude drift < 0.5° and background shift < 3 px [1.5 px] Normal
framing_drifted 0.5–1.0° or 3–12 px [1.5–6 px] Amber HUD, shots still captured, sidecar records motion.maxBackgroundShiftPx; the server downgrades the tier via c_frame
framing_lost > 1.0° or > 12 px [> 6 px] or shock Auto-pause recording, push the re-verify flow

The re-verify flow is deliberately not a full re-registration. The field is unchanged; only the camera moved:

framing_lost
   │
   ▼
[ReverifyFramingScreen]  live preview
   • the stored referenceFrameGray ghosted at 40% opacity
   • the stored goalQuadPx drawn as an outline
   • live readout: "background shift 18 px · tilt +1.4°"
   │
   ├── re-aim; background shift < 3 px sustained 1.5 s
   │      └─► re-lock: SAME field_profile_id, NEW camera_placement_id
   │          (attitude + reference frame refreshed; goal dims, ball size,
   │           shooter distance, goal geometry all reused)
   │          → resume recording, shots continue in the SAME cohort
   │
   └── cannot match within 60 s, OR the reprojected goal quad is > 2% off
          └─► full re-registration (the §5.1 walk)
              → NEW camera_placement_id AND a cohort-key change:
                shots before and after are NOT compared to each other

A re-lock does not restore ARKit pose — the ARSession is gone. It restores the visual and attitude baseline, which is what T_G_from_C's continued validity rests on. If the user physically moved the tripod, the framing match fails the 2 % goal-quad check and routes to the full walk, which is the correct outcome. Shots captured while framing_drifted upload with the drift in the sidecar and show an "unstable camera" chip; whether they rank is the server's call from c_frame and the cohort key, not the app's.

Backstop for any clip that arrives without a sidecar (a Quick-mode import): stabilize() reports pan["max_pair_pan_px"] and analyze_frames warns above 3 px (camera_pan_compensated). That is a quality flag, not a threshold — it fires on compensated pan, which is the stabilizer working, not failing.

Records only compare within one field_profile_id + one camera_placement hash — the brief's "only compare records under identical conditions".

Server-side staleness and the field-profile lifecycle. fields.service.staleness(profile, now, app_build) returns fresh | stale, and it is stale when any of: last_verified_at older than 30 days · calibration_schema_version below the current constant · device_model differs from the session's · goal dimensions edited since last_verified_at · more than 3 consecutive shots flagged camera_moved_since_calibration. A session on a stale profile is accepted, and every shot in it is written with Quick semantics for eligibility (field_profile_stale, is_verified=false). Re-verifying does not retroactively promote them.

draft ──POST /fields/{id}/framing (accepted)──▶ draft ──POST /fields/{id}/activate──▶ active
active ──calibration-check verdict=recalibrate_required──▶ needs_recalibration
active ──calibration-check verdict=reframe_only──▶ active   (last_verified_at unchanged)
needs_recalibration ──POST /framing (trigger='reverify') + POST /activate──▶ active
active|needs_recalibration ──POST /fields/{id}/retire──▶ retired  [terminal]

/activate has four server-side preconditions, each returning a Problem with its own code: FramingOut.blocking empty · closure_width_err_pct <= 5.0 (above 5 % c_cal is 0, so the shot could never be Verified-eligible anyway — refusing at registration saves the user a wasted session) · goal_corners_px.non_coplanar true · t_g_from_w present. Activating without t_g_from_w is permitted only for Quick-only profiles, which are marked expected_tier='C' and cannot host a Ranked session.

The server's own drift verdict is separate from the client's and the two are not the same threshold: ok at ≤ 6 px goal-corner drift, reframe_only at 6–25 px (keep the profile), recalibrate_required above 25 px or > 3° IMU rotation. The client's is stricter and fires first; this one decides ranking.

06 Data models

SQLAlchemy 2.0, Mapped[T] = mapped_column(...) throughout — the only style that is pyright-clean. JSONB is the Postgres dialect type; all timestamps are DateTime(timezone=True), UTC. The scaffold shipped training_sessions / attempts; v1 renames them to training_session / shot in the first migration — singular table names everywhere, and "attempt" becomes "shot" because that is the word the whole product uses.

6.1 accounts

class UserProfile(Base):
    __tablename__ = "user_profile"
    id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(primary_key=True)      # == Supabase auth.users.id; never generated here
    display_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40))
    handle: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), unique=True)
    dominant_foot: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(5))            # right | left | both
    birth_year: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)   # NOT age: the band is derived
    height_cm: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)    # pose-scale prior
    ball_size: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=5)               # 3 | 4 | 5
    ball_diameter_m: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.220)
    #   0.190 (3) | 0.205 (4) | 0.220 (5). A default HERE is fine — this is a user-editable row the
    #   user was shown. There is NO default in the wire contract: an unset value must be a
    #   validation error at the pipeline boundary, never a plausible constant.
    country: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(3), nullable=True)
    verification_level: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="registered")
    #   registered | verified | suspended        ('anonymous' is NOT a level — nothing can write it)
    verified_at / suspended_at: Mapped[datetime | None]
    suspended_reason: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(40), nullable=True)
    ranking_opt_in: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True)
    strike_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)             # denormalised, for gating
    created_at / updated_at: Mapped[datetime]
    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint("dominant_foot IN ('right','left','both')", name="ck_profile_foot"),
        CheckConstraint("ball_size IN (3,4,5)", name="ck_profile_ball_size"),
        CheckConstraint("verification_level IN ('registered','verified','suspended')",
                        name="ck_profile_verification"),
    )

class ProfileChangeLog(Base):
    """birth_year and dominant_foot both move a user between cohorts — the cheapest ranking exploit."""
    __tablename__ = "profile_change_log"
    id, user_id (FK, indexed)
    field: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24))         # birth_year | dominant_foot | ball_size
    old_value: Mapped[str | None]; new_value: Mapped[str]
    changed_at: Mapped[datetime]
    applied_to_history: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)  # admin-approved only
    reviewed_by: Mapped[str | None]

class DeletionRequest(Base):
    """The delete path must actually delete OBJECTS, not just rows."""
    __tablename__ = "deletion_request"
    id, user_id (FK, unique)
    requested_at: Mapped[datetime]
    grace_until: Mapped[datetime]                          # +7 days, cancellable
    rows_purged_at: Mapped[datetime | None]
    objects_purged_at: Mapped[datetime | None]
    objects_purged_count: Mapped[int | None]
    #   The sweep lists every bucket under the `{user_id}/` prefix and deletes it. The request is
    #   NOT closed until BOTH timestamps are set — a row-only delete leaves the clips behind, and
    #   "we deleted your account" would then be a lie the schema permitted.

6.2 fields

The field profile is the single most valuable row in the system: it carries the metric reference that moves ball speed from band-only to a point value, it is the object identity behind "only compare records under identical conditions", and it is what makes G recoverable across sessions.

class FieldProfile(Base):
    __tablename__ = "field_profile"
    id, user_id (FK, indexed)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(60))                     # "Hangang pitch 3"
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), default="draft")
    #   draft | active | needs_recalibration | retired

    # --- goal ---------------------------------------------------------------
    goal_preset: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))               # full | futsal | mini | custom
    goal_width_m: Mapped[float]; goal_height_m: Mapped[float]
    goal_corners_px: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    #   {"image_w":.., "image_h":.., "points":[{"name":"crossbar_left","u":..,"v":..}, …],
    #    "non_coplanar": true}   <- crossbar tops + post bases; coplanar-only PnP is degenerate

    # --- ball ---------------------------------------------------------------
    ball_size: Mapped[int]                                             # no default here
    ball_diameter_m: Mapped[float]                                     # copied at registration, then frozen

    # --- camera -------------------------------------------------------------
    device_model: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64))              # "iPhone16,2"
    lens: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="wide")
    mount: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))                     # tripod | braced | handheld
    camera_height_m / camera_distance_m / camera_azimuth_deg: Mapped[float | None]
    #   azimuth: angle between the camera axis and the shot line. 90 = side-on; <25 = down-the-axis
    intrinsics_source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24))
    #   arkit_per_frame | in_app_capture | goal_pnp | device_db | diagonal_heuristic
    intrinsics: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(JSONB)                    # {fx,fy,cx,cy,distortion[],readout_us}
    has_lidar: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)    # aid, never a requirement

    # --- the transform that makes G recoverable -----------------------------
    t_g_from_w: Mapped[dict | None] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=True)
    #   4x4 row-major, the goal frame expressed in the ARKit world of registration.
    #   NULL => this profile cannot fix camera height/tilt, so Verified-mode parameter reduction
    #   does not apply and /activate refuses it for ranked use.
    post_bases_w / post_tops_w / ground_plane_w / camera_placement: Mapped[dict | None]
    closure_width_m / closure_width_err_pct: Mapped[float | None]
    closure_height_m / closure_height_err_pct: Mapped[float | None]
    #   Re-measuring the goal we just anchored against the dimensions the user declared. This is the
    #   number that decides whether the field is Verified-grade: it is returned by FramingOut and
    #   FieldProfileOut, it is an /activate precondition, it feeds c_cal, and it is the quantity
    #   gate A10 measures at the 90th percentile.
    calibration_schema_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1)
    #   Bumped when an app build changes how any of the geometry above is computed. A profile below
    #   the current version is stale regardless of its age.

    # --- shooting zones -----------------------------------------------------
    zone_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20))   # free_kick_circle | dribble_zone | touch_pair
    shot_zone: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(JSONB)       # polygon/circle in G, metres
    touch_zone: Mapped[dict | None]                      # touch_pair only
    nominal_distance_m: Mapped[float]                    # zone centroid -> goal centre. A FALLBACK ONLY.

    # --- identity of "identical conditions" ---------------------------------
    calibration_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)
    #   sha256 over the quantised tuple:
    #   (goal_w, goal_h, ball_size, round(nominal_distance_m*2)/2,
    #    round(camera_azimuth_deg/15)*15, zone_kind, device_model, lens, calibration_schema_version)
    #   Two sessions comparable <=> same calibration_hash. Recomputed on every PATCH.

    reference_frame_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime]; last_verified_at: Mapped[datetime | None]
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("ix_field_user_status", "user_id", "status"),
        CheckConstraint("ball_size IN (3,4,5)", name="ck_field_ball_size"),
        CheckConstraint("zone_kind IN ('free_kick_circle','dribble_zone','touch_pair')",
                        name="ck_field_zone"),
    )

class FieldCalibrationCheck(Base):
    """What makes 'auto-detect camera movement -> recalibrate' auditable, and the evidence for the
    camera_moved ranking invalidation."""
    __tablename__ = "field_calibration_check"
    id, field_profile_id (FK, indexed), session_id (FK, indexed, nullable)
    checked_at: Mapped[datetime]
    trigger: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))     # session_start | periodic | client_alert | reverify
    frame_key: Mapped[str | None]
    goal_corner_drift_px: Mapped[float]
    imu_rotation_deg: Mapped[float | None]
    closure_width_err_pct: Mapped[float | None]
    verdict: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24))
    #   ok (<= 6 px) | reframe_only (6-25 px, keep the profile) | recalibrate_required (>25 px or >3 deg)
    from_shot_sequence: Mapped[int | None]               # first suspect shot; NULL if the check passed
    hints: Mapped[list] = mapped_column(JSONB, default=list)

The persisted field-profile geometry, as the ritual writes it:

{
  "id": "…", "created_at": "…", "device_model": "iPhone15,3",
  "goal": { "preset": "full", "width_m": 7.32, "height_m": 2.44, "source": "user_confirmed_preset" },
  "T_G_from_W": [[...4x4...]],
  "post_bases_w":  { "left": [x,y,z], "right": [x,y,z] },
  "post_tops_w":   { "left": [x,y,z], "right": [x,y,z] },
  "closure_residual": { "width_m": 7.29, "width_err_pct": 0.41,
                        "height_m": 2.47, "height_err_pct": 1.23,
                        "closure_grade": "good" },        // good | degraded | rejected. NOT a tier letter.
  "ground_plane_w": { "normal": [0,1,0], "d": 0.0, "source": "arkit_plane|lidar_mesh" },
  "shooting_zones": [ {"kind":"free_kick_circle","polygon_g":[[x,z],…]},
                      {"kind":"dribble_zone","polygon_g":[[x,z],…]} ],
  "camera_placement": { "position_g": [x,y,z], "look_at_g": [x,y,z],
                        "height_m": 1.55, "azimuth_off_axis_deg": 42.0 },
  "intrinsics": { "fx": 2846.1, "fy": 2846.1, "cx": 1919.5, "cy": 1079.5,
                  "source": "avfoundation_per_frame",     // ARKit K is the RITUAL-TIME cross-check
                  "arkit_ritual_fx": 1421.4,              // recorded, compared, never the capture K
                  "capture_mode": "4K60" }
}

Per session ARKit relocalises into a world W', the app resolves T_W'_from_W by re-observing the goal, and T_G_from_C(t) = T_G_from_W · T_W_from_W' · T_W'_from_C(t). The fit then runs directly in G, and B disappears from the Ranked path entirely — this is the structural difference between the modes, not a UI label:

Fit parameter Quick Ranked
camera height h free (0.8–4.0 m) fixed from T_G_from_C
camera tilt free (0–45°) fixed from T_G_from_C
focal f diagonal heuristic ±15 % fixed from per-frame intrinsics
launch speed v0 free free
elevation, azimuth free free
g_eff free (3–20 m/s²) fixed at 9.81
lateral curl aL free (shape pass) free (shape pass)
free parameters 7 3 (+1 curl)

6.3 training

class TrainingSession(Base):
    __tablename__ = "training_session"
    id, user_id (FK, indexed)
    mode: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8))                     # quick | ranked
    field_profile_id: Mapped[UUID | None] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("field_profile.id"),
                                                          nullable=True, index=True)  # NULL only when quick
    calibration_hash: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True, nullable=True)
    #   snapshotted at start; a later edit to the profile must not retroactively re-cohort old sessions
    field_stale_at_start: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)

    # --- Quick Mode's frame of reference. Without these, every quick job shipped goal=None
    #     (permanent band-only) and a defaulted size-5 ball. -----------------
    goal_preset / goal_width_m / goal_height_m: Mapped[… | None]
    ball_size: Mapped[int]                    # no default; from StartSessionIn
    ball_diameter_m: Mapped[float]            # no default
    declared_distance_m: Mapped[float | None] # the user-editable auto-estimate

    state: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="created")  # created|active|completed|abandoned
    shot_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    device_model: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64))
    device_install_id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(index=True)
    #   identifierForVendor-derived, stable per app install. The ONLY device identity we have;
    #   `device_model` is a string shared by millions of phones and cannot carry a per-device limit.
    app_version / app_build
    capture_fps_nominal: Mapped[float | None]                         # 60 | 120 | 240
    capture_source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="in_app")   # in_app | photo_library
    challenge_jk_record_id: Mapped[UUID | None] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("jk_record.id"), nullable=True)
    started_at / completed_at
    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint("mode IN ('quick','ranked')", name="ck_session_mode"),
        CheckConstraint("mode = 'quick' OR field_profile_id IS NOT NULL", name="ck_ranked_needs_field"),
        CheckConstraint("ball_size IN (3,4,5)", name="ck_session_ball_size"),
        Index("ix_session_user_started", "user_id", "started_at"),
        Index("ix_session_device_started", "device_install_id", "started_at"),
    )


class Shot(Base):
    """The spine. One row per kick. Everything else keys off shot.id."""
    __tablename__ = "shot"
    id, session_id (FK, indexed), user_id (FK, indexed, denormalised)
    field_profile_id: Mapped[UUID | None] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("field_profile.id"),
                                                          nullable=True, index=True)
    sequence: Mapped[int]                                   # 1-based within the session
    mode: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8))            # copied from the session
    calibration_hash: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True, nullable=True)

    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20), default="pending_upload", index=True)
    #   pending_upload | uploading | uploaded | queued | analysing | partial
    #   | ready | refused | failed | invalidated
    #   Stored, denormalised, and written by EXACTLY ONE function,
    #   training.service.recompute_shot_status(shot_id). Stored because ix_shot_cohort_scan
    #   indexes it; a derived-only value cannot be indexed.

    captured_at: Mapped[datetime]; duration_s: Mapped[float | None]

    # --- the clip, and the anti-replay identity -----------------------------
    clip_artifact_id: Mapped[UUID | None] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("artifact.id"), nullable=True, index=True)
    #   kind='clip_shot'; the per-shot passthrough clip the client uploads
    crop_artifact_id: Mapped[UUID | None] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("artifact.id"), nullable=True)
    #   kind='clip_playback'; the worker's re-encoded playback copy. NEVER analysed.
    source_asset_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(80), nullable=True)
    window_start_s / window_end_s: Mapped[float | None]
    window_fingerprint: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    #   sha256(f"{source_asset_id}:{round(window_start_s,2)}:{round(window_end_s,2)}")
    #   artifact's UNIQUE(user_id, sha256) catches identical BYTES; this catches the cheaper attack
    #   of re-EXPORTING the same window (different bytes, same kick).

    # --- geometry of this kick ---------------------------------------------
    shot_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(12))      # free_kick | dribble | first_touch
    target_zone: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    #   the 3x3 mouth cell the user aimed at, declared BEFORE the kick. Required for
    #   placement_error_m to be a metric at all; NULL = no placement ranking.
    foot: Mapped[str | None]; foot_source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(12), default="declared")
    #   declared | pose.  pose.kicking_foot OVERWRITES shot.foot when the pose lane succeeds;
    #   a `declared` foot does not rank.
    distance_m: Mapped[float | None]
    distance_source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(12), default="estimated")
    #   fitted   = hypot(X,Z) of the fitted launch point in G. REQUIRED for ranked shots.
    #   measured = field_profile.nominal_distance_m (zone centroid). Fallback/display only.
    #   estimated = Quick Mode auto-estimate. Drives the "Estimated" label AND ranking exclusion.
    launch_in_zone: Mapped[bool | None]
    #   the fitted launch point tested against field_profile.shot_zone. NULL when there is no
    #   fitted launch point or no zone. False => not ranked.

    # --- headline numbers (all nullable BY DESIGN) --------------------------
    ball_speed_kmh: Mapped[float | None]
    ball_speed_low_kmh / ball_speed_high_kmh: Mapped[float | None]
    claim_point_speed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    arrival_x_m / arrival_y_m: Mapped[float | None]         # goal frame G; y = 0 is the ground
    on_target: Mapped[bool | None]
    placement_zone: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)

    # --- confidence: TWO scores, one owner, inputs stored -------------------
    speed_confidence: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    pose_confidence:  Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float, nullable=True)
    #   Computed in core/confidence.py at shot settle, once BOTH lanes are terminal.
    #   NULL means not yet computed — never 0.0, which would read as "measured and bad".
    confidence_inputs: Mapped[dict | None] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=True)
    #   {"c_ref":1.0,"c_time":0.94,"c_det":1.0,"c_rms":0.81,"c_basin":0.25,"c_anchor":null,
    #    "c_rail":1.0,"c_frame":1.0,"c_cal":1.0,"c_pose":1.0,"c_kp":0.88,"c_subj":1.0}
    #   EVERY term present as a value or an EXPLICIT null — never a neutral default.
    #   A null hard term means the score is not computable yet, not that the term passed.
    tier: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(1), nullable=True)      # A | B | C | D
    #   A pure relabel of speed_confidence's four bands, for chips and copy.
    #   Nothing gates on the letter; the gates use the float.
    quality_flags: Mapped[list] = mapped_column(JSONB, default=list)
    invalidated_reason: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    #   camera_moved | duplicate_clip | field_edited | user_deleted | abuse_review | dispute_upheld
    created_at / updated_at

    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("session_id", "sequence", name="uq_shot_session_sequence"),
        UniqueConstraint("user_id", "window_fingerprint", name="uq_shot_user_window"),
        Index("ix_shot_user_captured", "user_id", "captured_at"),
        Index("ix_shot_cohort_scan", "calibration_hash", "mode", "status"),
        CheckConstraint("shot_type IN ('free_kick','dribble','first_touch')", name="ck_shot_type"),
        CheckConstraint("foot_source IN ('declared','pose')", name="ck_shot_foot_source"),
        CheckConstraint("distance_source IN ('fitted','measured','estimated')",
                        name="ck_shot_distance_source"),
        CheckConstraint("ball_speed_kmh IS NULL OR claim_point_speed IS TRUE",
                        name="ck_shot_no_unclaimed_speed"),          # the honesty invariant, in Postgres
        CheckConstraint("ball_speed_low_kmh IS NULL OR ball_speed_high_kmh >= ball_speed_low_kmh",
                        name="ck_shot_band_ordered"),
        CheckConstraint("speed_confidence IS NULL OR confidence_inputs IS NOT NULL",
                        name="ck_shot_confidence_shows_its_work"),   # a score with no inputs is not auditable
    )

6.4 analysis

class Trajectory(Base):
    """One row per shot. Mirrors ball_flight.json field-for-field so the mapper is a rename."""
    __tablename__ = "trajectory"
    shot_id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("shot.id"), primary_key=True)
    pipeline_version: Mapped[str]; schema_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=3)

    frame: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(1))            # "G" | "B"
    #   "G" = goal frame — the ONLY frame the shot map may render, and the only one in which two
    #         shots at one field are co-renderable.
    #   "B" = the ball-fit frame, origin at the launch point, "up" only because the solver's tilt
    #         came out right. Renderable ALONE over its own video, NEVER on a multi-shot map.
    points_3d: Mapped[list] = mapped_column(JSONB)            # [{"t":..,"x":..,"y":..,"z":..}, …]
    points_decimated: Mapped[list] = mapped_column(JSONB)     # <= 24 points — what the map renders
    n_points / n_detections / n_frames_tracked: Mapped[int]   # n_detections < 8 => refusal
    contact_frame: Mapped[int | None]
    contact_source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20))
    #   ball_departure | foot_kinematics | reconciled | disagreed
    contact_disagreement_ms: Mapped[float | None]             # in MILLISECONDS (17 / 33 ms bands)

    # --- fit ----------------------------------------------------------------
    fit_rms_px: Mapped[float]; fit_model: Mapped[str]; drag_model: Mapped[str]
    magnus_dofs: Mapped[int]; effective_g_ms2: Mapped[float]  # NOT a spin measurement
    free_parameters: Mapped[int]                              # 7 quick / 3 (+1 curl) ranked
    launch_point: Mapped[dict | None]                         # {x,y,z} in `frame`
    launch_elevation_deg / launch_azimuth_deg / apex_m / carry_m: Mapped[float | None]
    goal_crossing: Mapped[dict | None]                        # {x_m,y_m,t_s,on_target} at first Z_G=0
    #   NULL when frame='B' (no goal plane exists) or the ball never reached the plane.

    # --- bands, per anchor and overall --------------------------------------
    speed_band_low_kmh / speed_band_high_kmh: Mapped[float]   # ALWAYS present
    per_anchor: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    #   {"goal":{"kmh":41.2,"band":[39.0,43.5],"used":true},
    #    "diameter":{"kmh":37.1,"band":[30.1,44.8],"used":true},
    #    "motion_plane":{"kmh":null,"band":null,"used":false}}
    #   Anchor priority goal > diameter. THE GRAVITY RULER IS GONE (2026-08-21, commit 36d6aa0,
    #   solved g_eff 14.69 m/s^2). Do not re-add a "gravity" key.
    anchor_spread_pct: Mapped[float | None]

    # --- the degeneracy, reported not hidden --------------------------------
    basin_ambiguous: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True)
    basin_solutions: Mapped[list] = mapped_column(JSONB, default=list)
    #   every surviving camera geometry: rank / rms_px / speed_kmh_central / elevation_deg /
    #   apex_m / camera{height_m, tilt_deg}
    railed_parameters: Mapped[list] = mapped_column(JSONB, default=list)
    #   solved values that hit a bound. A railed fit is a REJECTED fit in v1: c_rail = 0.

    # --- spin: qualitative only ---------------------------------------------
    spin_type: Mapped[str | None]        # topspin | backspin | flat
    curl_direction: Mapped[str | None]   # left | right | none
    spin_confidence: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), default="low")
    #   NO spin magnitude column, ever. Hawk-Eye's own multi-camera spin SD is +-542 RPM.

    camera: Mapped[dict]; timebase: Mapped[dict]
    claim_point_speed / claim_apex / claim_elevation: Mapped[bool]
    speed_refusal_reason / apex_refusal_reason: Mapped[str | None]
    metric_reference: Mapped[bool]
    overlay_artifact_id / strip_artifact_id: Mapped[UUID | None]
    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint("frame IN ('G','B')", name="ck_traj_frame"),
        CheckConstraint("apex_m IS NULL OR claim_apex IS TRUE", name="ck_traj_no_unclaimed_apex"),
        CheckConstraint("launch_elevation_deg IS NULL OR claim_elevation IS TRUE",
                        name="ck_traj_no_unclaimed_elevation"),
        CheckConstraint("goal_crossing IS NULL OR frame = 'G'",
                        name="ck_traj_crossing_needs_goal_frame"),
    )


class Pose(Base):
    __tablename__ = "pose"
    shot_id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("shot.id"), primary_key=True)
    model_name / model_version: Mapped[str]
    body_model: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))       # smplx | mhr | gemx
    model_license_class: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))   # noncommercial | commercial
    #   Every PUBLIC/commercial surface (share render, leaderboard card, marketing export) checks
    #   this column. Private in-app viewing does not.
    mesh_format: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), default="glb")
    mesh_artifact_id / params_artifact_id: Mapped[UUID | None]
    mesh_fps: Mapped[float]; mesh_frame_count: Mapped[int]
    kicker_person_index: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    kicker_fully_in_frame: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True)
    #   False => NO posture metrics at all. A mesh fitted to a cropped body is the
    #   "default read as a measurement" bug wearing a 3D costume.

    # --- impact posture (flat: this IS the KickProfile the compare screens read) ---
    contact_frame: Mapped[int | None]; contact_time_s: Mapped[float | None]
    kicking_foot: Mapped[str | None]        # AUTHORITATIVE; copied into shot.foot, foot_source='pose'
    peak_foot_speed_ms / peak_ankle_speed_ms / peak_knee_speed_ms: Mapped[float | None]
    approach_speed_kmh: Mapped[float | None]
    knee_deg / support_knee_deg / hip_deg / arm_angle_deg: Mapped[float | None]
    trunk_lean_deg / shoulder_hip_rotation_deg: Mapped[float | None]
    metrics_confidence: Mapped[float | None]
    # NO overall_score column, ever. Per-item only.


class Artifact(Base):
    """One row per stored file, so retention, quota, GDPR deletion and signed-URL minting have a
    single code path."""
    __tablename__ = "artifact"
    id, shot_id (FK, nullable, indexed), session_id (FK, nullable, indexed), user_id (FK, indexed)
    kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), index=True)   # values are ArtifactKind in contracts/
    bucket / object_key (unique) / content_type
    bytes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger)
    sha256: Mapped[str | None]; expires_at: Mapped[datetime | None]; created_at
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uq_artifact_user_clip_sha", "user_id", "sha256", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("kind = 'clip_shot' AND sha256 IS NOT NULL")),
        #   The anti-replay control: the same bytes can never be ingested twice by one user.
        #   Scoped to clip_shot so a deterministic worker render colliding on sha is not an error.
        Index("ix_artifact_user_kind", "user_id", "kind"),
    )


class UploadSession(Base):
    __tablename__ = "upload_session"
    id, user_id (FK), shot_id (FK), artifact_id (FK)
    state: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="open", index=True)
    #   open | completing | completed | abandoned | aborted
    declared_bytes: Mapped[int]; declared_sha256: Mapped[str]
    part_size_bytes: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=8 * 1024 * 1024)
    part_count: Mapped[int]; provider_upload_id: Mapped[str]
    created_at; last_part_at (indexed); completed_at; abandon_reason

class UploadPart(Base):
    __tablename__ = "upload_part"
    upload_session_id (PK, FK); part_number (PK)     # 1-based, S3 convention
    etag: Mapped[str | None]; bytes: Mapped[int | None]; committed_at: Mapped[datetime | None]

Abandoned-upload reaper, one asyncio task, tick every 60 s:

UPDATE upload_session SET state = 'abandoned', abandon_reason = 'no_complete_within_15m'
 WHERE state = 'open' AND last_part_at IS NOT NULL
   AND last_part_at < now() - interval '15 minutes';

An abandoned session aborts the provider-side multipart upload (so the parts stop being billed), sets shot.status='pending_upload', and is resumable: POST /uploads with the same Idempotency-Key re-opens it and returns the committed part numbers. Only after three abandonments in 24 h does the client show terminal copy.

class AnalysisJob(Base):
    __tablename__ = "analysis_job"
    id, shot_id (FK, indexed), session_id (FK, indexed), user_id (FK, indexed)
    lane: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8))        # ball | pose | render
    state: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), index=True, default="queued")
    #   queued | dispatched | running | retry_wait | cancelling
    #   | succeeded | refused | failed | cancelled
    #   EVERY transition is written by be-api. The worker writes none.
    contract_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), default="2")
    idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(96), unique=True)
    #   sha256(f"{shot_id}:{clip_sha256}:{lane}:{pipeline_version}:{attempt}")
    #   `attempt` is in the key so a manual retry is a NEW job rather than a 200 returning the
    #   failed one; `pipeline_version` is in it so a pipeline release re-analyses.
    priority: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=100)
    attempt: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1)
    max_attempts: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=3)
    manual_retries: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)     # capped at 2
    stage_index / stage / stage_count                                   # stage_count: no default
    progress_pct: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.0)
    worker_id: Mapped[str | None]; provider_call_id: Mapped[str | None]  # so cancel can cancel
    lease_expires_at (indexed) / heartbeat_at / queued_at / dispatched_at / started_at / finished_at
    deadline_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(index=True)
    failure_class: Mapped[str | None]      # input | infrastructure | contract — only infra retries
    failure_code / failure_detail: Mapped[str | None]
    refusal_reasons: Mapped[list] = mapped_column(JSONB, default=list)
    timings_ms: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(JSONB, default=dict)
    cold_start: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    __table_args__ = (Index("ix_job_state_deadline", "state", "deadline_at"),
                      Index("ix_job_shot_lane", "shot_id", "lane"),
                      CheckConstraint("lane IN ('ball','pose','render')", name="ck_job_lane"))


class ShotDispute(Base):
    __tablename__ = "shot_dispute"
    id, shot_id (FK, indexed), user_id (FK, indexed)
    field_profile_id: Mapped[UUID | None]     # denormalised: >3 disputes/week on ONE profile pages
                                              # the validation lead
    metric_key: Mapped[str]                   # ball_speed_kmh | placement | foot …
    reason: Mapped[str]                       # too_high|too_low|wrong_shot|wrong_foot|other
    user_claimed_value: Mapped[float | None]  # AN OPINION, NOT A MEASUREMENT
    note: Mapped[str | None]
    state: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="open", index=True)  # open|reviewing|upheld|rejected
    resolution_note / reviewed_by / created_at / resolved_at


class ValidationSample(Base):
    """A clip promoted into the validation corpus, with whatever ground truth we have."""
    __tablename__ = "validation_sample"
    id, shot_id (FK, unique)
    source: Mapped[str]                       # dispute | radar_run | synthetic
    run_id: Mapped[str | None]                # "2026-09-14-radar-run-1"
    truth_speed_kmh / truth_arrival_x_m / truth_arrival_y_m / truth_distance_m: Mapped[float | None]
    truth_source: Mapped[str | None]
    #   pocket_radar | tape_measure | user_claim
    #   'user_claim' is NOT ground truth and is EXCLUDED from every acceptance-gate computation.
    clip_retained_until: Mapped[datetime | None]   # overrides the bucket lifecycle
    created_at

Upholding a dispute writes shot.invalidated_reason='dispute_upheld', re-runs the eligibility evaluator, supersedes any personal best it set, and notifies the user. The stored number is never edited in place — "never silently edit a number the user has already seen" is honoured by invalidating the shot, not by rewriting it.

6.5 insights, progress, rankings, jk, challenges

class Insight(Base):
    __tablename__ = "insight"
    id, user_id (FK, indexed)
    kind: Mapped[str]                 # posture | consistency | placement | speed
    metric_key: Mapped[str]           # "trunk_lean_deg"
    template_key: Mapped[str]         # "posture.trunk_lean_back.limiting"
    #   Copy lives in the i18n catalogue, NOT in this table. There is no free-text body column —
    #   that is how the wording rule stays guaranteed and translatable.
    template_params: Mapped[dict]     # {"n":7,"k":6,"metric":"trunk_lean","prescription_shots":3}

    cell_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), index=True)
    #   core.cohort.cell_key(field_profile_id, foot, shot_type, distance_bin_2m) — the SAME key
    #   function the ranking cohort uses. Without this column the window is user-global.
    field_profile_id: Mapped[UUID | None]

    window_shots: Mapped[int]         # n, eligible shots in the cell
    flagged_shots: Mapped[int]        # k
    p0: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.30)   # stored per insight, so a later
                                                             # re-measurement does not restate old cards
    binomial_p: Mapped[float]
    holm_threshold: Mapped[float]     # alpha / M at this insight's rank
    median_abs_z: Mapped[float]
    outcome_weight: Mapped[float]     # w_m
    outcome_weight_source: Mapped[str]   # jk_reference | regression
    #   'jk_reference' is UNMEASURED and the copy layer MUST say so.
    impact_score: Mapped[float]       # w_m * median_abs_z * (k/n)
    baseline_source: Mapped[str]      # user | population
    window_start_at / window_end_at

    is_primary: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="active")
    #   active | dismissed | improved | no_change | inconclusive | superseded | expired
    #   Writers, one per value — an unreachable status is a lie in a dropdown.
    expires_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(index=True)      # issued_at + 30 days
    jk_lesson_id: Mapped[UUID | None]
    followup_shots_required: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=3)
    followup_shots_seen: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    followup_result: Mapped[dict | None]
    #   {"metric_delta":-6.1,"speed_delta_kmh":2.1,"placement_delta_m":-0.18,
    #    "consistency_delta":0.22,"verdict":"improved"|"unchanged"|"worse"}
    #   "The metric moved but the outcome did not" is a legitimate verdict and must be renderable.
    issued_at / resolved_at
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("uq_insight_one_primary", "user_id", unique=True,
              postgresql_where=text("is_primary AND status = 'active'")),
        #   "recommend only the single highest-impact issue", enforced by the database.
        CheckConstraint("flagged_shots <= window_shots", name="ck_insight_k_le_n"),
        CheckConstraint("window_shots >= 7", name="ck_insight_min_window"),   # the hard floor
    )

class InsightShot(Base):
    __tablename__ = "insight_shot"
    insight_id (PK, FK); shot_id (PK, FK)
    role: Mapped[str]                 # flagged | unflagged | followup
    #   The `unflagged` runner-up rows are M5's control group, so the engine MUST persist them.
    value: Mapped[float | None]; z: Mapped[float | None]


class Benchmark(Base):
    """personal | jk | stryke_verified — one shape, three scopes, one 'vs' row."""
    __tablename__ = "benchmark"
    id; scope: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), index=True)
    user_id: Mapped[UUID | None]            # NULL for jk / stryke_verified
    cohort_key / calibration_hash: Mapped[str | None]  # for jk: the conditions the record was set under
    metric_key: Mapped[str]; value: Mapped[float]; unit: Mapped[str]
    direction: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(4), default="high")
    source_shot_id: Mapped[UUID | None]     # personal bests always point at the shot that set them
    sample_size: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=1)
    confidence: Mapped[float | None]
    is_verified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(12), default="measured")   # measured | curated
    provenance: Mapped[str | None]          # who measured it, with what, when. RENDERED INLINE.
    valid_from / valid_to
    __table_args__ = (Index("uq_benchmark_current", "scope","user_id","cohort_key","metric_key",
                            unique=True, postgresql_where=text("valid_to IS NULL")),)

Benchmarks are append-with-supersede: a new personal best closes the old row (valid_to = now()) and inserts a new one. That gives "delta vs personal best" history for free, and it is how an invalidated shot's personal best is withdrawn without deleting evidence.

class LeaderboardEligibility(Base):
    __tablename__ = "leaderboard_eligibility"
    shot_id (PK, FK); user_id (FK, indexed)
    is_verified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(index=True, default=False)   # THE flag
    cohort_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), index=True)
    cohort_dims: Mapped[dict]
    #   {"dist_band":"16-18","goal":"full","shot_type":"free_kick","foot":"right",
    #    "foot_source":"pose","age_band":"18-29"}
    age_band_snapshot: Mapped[str | None]     # SNAPSHOTTED, exactly as calibration_hash is
    calibration_hash: Mapped[str | None]
    speed_confidence: Mapped[float]
    confidence_threshold: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.75)
    #   Stored per row so a threshold change does not silently restate old verdicts.
    band_width_pct: Mapped[float | None]      # (high-low)/mid*100. Ranked speed requires <= 20.
    eligible_metrics: Mapped[list] = mapped_column(JSONB, default=list)
    #   PER-METRIC, because eligibility is not all-or-nothing: trajectory SHAPE is one tier more
    #   robust than the speed number, so placement and consistency can rank on a band-only clip.
    reasons: Mapped[list] = mapped_column(JSONB, default=list)
    #   machine-readable, ORDERED BY FIXABILITY so the app can coach:
    #   [{"code":"quick_mode","fixable":true,"hint":"register_field"},
    #    {"code":"not_in_app_capture","fixable":true,"hint":"record_in_stryke"},
    #    {"code":"screen_recording_suspected","fixable":false},
    #    {"code":"no_metric_reference","fixable":true,"hint":"get_goal_in_frame"},
    #    {"code":"down_the_axis","fixable":true,"hint":"film_from_the_side"},
    #    {"code":"foot_unverified","fixable":true,"hint":"none"},
    #    {"code":"low_confidence","fixable":true,"hint":"more_light"},
    #    {"code":"launch_outside_zone","fixable":true,"hint":"shoot_from_the_zone"},
    #    {"code":"field_profile_stale","fixable":true,"hint":"reverify_framing"},
    #    {"code":"user_not_verified","fixable":true,"hint":"verify_email"},
    #    {"code":"camera_moved","fixable":false},
    #    {"code":"review_pending","fixable":false},
    #    {"code":"rankings_not_launched","fixable":false}]
    state: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default="evaluated", index=True)
    #   evaluated | review_pending | invalidated
    evaluator_version / evaluated_at / invalidated_at / invalidated_reason
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("ix_elig_cohort_verified", "cohort_key", "is_verified", "invalidated_at"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "is_verified IS FALSE OR (cohort_key IS NOT NULL AND age_band_snapshot IS NOT NULL"
            " AND calibration_hash IS NOT NULL AND speed_confidence >= confidence_threshold)",
            name="ck_elig_verified_has_cohort"),
        #   Ranked-mode integrity is a CONSTRAINT, not a convention inside an evaluator.
    )

-- plus a trigger, because the remaining preconditions live on other tables
CREATE FUNCTION assert_verified_shot_is_rankable() RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.is_verified THEN
    PERFORM 1 FROM shot s
      JOIN training_session ts ON ts.id = s.session_id
      JOIN user_profile u ON u.id = s.user_id
     WHERE s.id = NEW.shot_id
       AND s.mode = 'ranked' AND s.distance_source = 'fitted' AND s.launch_in_zone IS TRUE
       AND s.invalidated_reason IS NULL AND s.foot_source = 'pose'
       AND ts.capture_source = 'in_app'
       AND u.verification_level = 'verified' AND u.ranking_opt_in;
    IF NOT FOUND THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'is_verified set on a shot that is not rankable: %', NEW.shot_id;
    END IF;
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
CREATE TRIGGER trg_elig_verified BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON leaderboard_eligibility
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION assert_verified_shot_is_rankable();

class CohortPercentile(Base):
    __tablename__ = "cohort_percentile"
    cohort_key (PK); metric_key (PK); direction: Mapped[str]
    breaks: Mapped[list] = mapped_column(JSONB)
    #   99 values: the 1st..99th percentile boundaries, ascending in RAW metric units. A shot's
    #   percentile is a binary search over this array at read time — no per-shot percentile rows,
    #   so nothing goes stale and two screens cannot disagree.
    sample_shots / sample_users / sample_shots_90d: Mapped[int]
    is_published: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    min_users_required: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=30)
    min_shots_required: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=200)
    min_shots_90d_required: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=50)
    parent_cohort_key: Mapped[str | None]
    computed_at; computed_by                 # "nightly" | "incremental"

class CohortDirty(Base):
    __tablename__ = "cohort_dirty"
    cohort_key (PK); metric_key (PK); new_shots: Mapped[int] = 0; marked_at


class JKLesson(Base):
    __tablename__ = "jk_lesson"
    id; slug (unique); title_key (i18n key, never prose); technique (indexed)
    duration_s: Mapped[int]                                  # ~300 for a 5-minute lesson
    video_artifact_id (FK); tip_artifact_id (FK, nullable)   # the 15-30 s result-screen cut
    chapters: Mapped[list]                                    # [{"t_s":0,"title_key":".."}]
    addresses_metric_keys: Mapped[list]                       # how an insight picks its lesson
    is_published / rights_cleared: Mapped[bool]

class JKRecord(Base):
    __tablename__ = "jk_record"
    id; label_key
    calibration_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), index=True)   # comparability = equality
    conditions: Mapped[dict]        # {goal_preset, goal_width_m, distance_m, dist_band, shot_type,
                                    #  foot, ball_size, venue}
    metric_key; value; band_low; band_high
    trajectory_points: Mapped[list]  # decimated, GOAL FRAME G only
    arrival_x_m / arrival_y_m: Mapped[float | None]
    clip_artifact_id: Mapped[UUID | None]
    provenance: Mapped[str]          # who measured it, with what, when — RENDERED INLINE
    measured_with: Mapped[str]       # "pocket_radar" | "stryke_pipeline"
    is_published: Mapped[bool]

class JKChallenge(Base):
    __tablename__ = "jk_challenge"
    id; user_id (FK); jk_record_id (FK); field_profile_id (FK); session_id (FK, nullable)
    conditions_match: Mapped[bool]   # calibration_hash equality, decided at creation
    state: Mapped[str]               # open | completed | abandoned
    best_shot_id / best_value
    result: Mapped[str | None]       # beaten | not_beaten | not_comparable
    __table_args__ = (CheckConstraint("conditions_match IS TRUE OR result IS DISTINCT FROM 'beaten'",
                                      name="ck_jk_no_beat_across_conditions"),)


class KeeperModel(Base):
    __tablename__ = "keeper_model"
    id; tier: Mapped[str]                   # elite | pro | club | youth
    display_name: Mapped[str]               # "Elite Keeper" — NOT a real name
    reaction_time_ms: Mapped[int]; dive_reach_m / vertical_reach_m: Mapped[float]
    params: Mapped[dict]
    rights_cleared: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    #   A named real keeper needs name + likeness + motion rights. Until this is true the row MUST
    #   be a generic tier. The API filters on this column; it is not a UI decision.
    rights_note: Mapped[str | None]; is_active: Mapped[bool]

class KeeperChallenge(Base):
    __tablename__ = "keeper_challenge"
    id; shot_id (FK, indexed); user_id (FK, indexed); keeper_model_id (FK)
    state: Mapped[str]                      # ready | ineligible — both terminal, both synchronous
    ineligible_reason: Mapped[str | None]   # no_trajectory | no_goal_crossing | frame_not_goal
    save_probability: Mapped[float | None]; outcome: Mapped[str | None]   # save | goal
    seed: Mapped[int]                       # deterministic replay of the sampled outcome
    sim_model_version: Mapped[str]
    statement_template_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(default="keeper.simulated_probability")
    #   Copy is a template, never a server-rendered sentence, so the claim is always framed as a
    #   model output under a named tier.
    created_at

6.6 Device-local schema (expo-sqlite, src/db/schema.sql)

CREATE TABLE sessions (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, mode TEXT NOT NULL,            -- 'quick' | 'ranked'
  field_profile_id TEXT, camera_placement_id TEXT,
  state TEXT NOT NULL,                                -- the session FSM
  format_rank INTEGER NOT NULL,
  started_at INTEGER NOT NULL, ended_at INTEGER, server_id TEXT
);
CREATE TABLE shots (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sessions(id),
  sequence INTEGER NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL,                               -- server spellings + device-local states
  trigger_source TEXT NOT NULL, trigger_confidence REAL NOT NULL,
  video_path TEXT NOT NULL, sidecar_path TEXT NOT NULL, thumbnail_path TEXT,
  bytes INTEGER NOT NULL, captured_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  framing_state TEXT NOT NULL,                        -- 'ok' | 'drifted' at capture time
  thermal_state_at_capture TEXT NOT NULL,             -- nominal|fair|serious|critical
  zone_checked INTEGER NOT NULL, zone_violation INTEGER NOT NULL,
  server_shot_id TEXT, ball_job_id TEXT, pose_job_id TEXT,
  result_json TEXT,                                   -- cached ShotDetailOut
  mesh_glb_path TEXT
);
CREATE TABLE field_profiles (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT NOT NULL,
  goal_width_m REAL NOT NULL, goal_height_m REAL NOT NULL, ball_size_cm REAL NOT NULL,
  shooting_zones_json TEXT,                           -- polygons in G, for the on-device zone test
  t_g_from_w TEXT,                                    -- 4x4, JSON
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL, server_id TEXT
);
CREATE TABLE camera_placements (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, field_profile_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES field_profiles(id),
  camera_height_m REAL, camera_tilt_deg REAL, azimuth_deg REAL, shooter_distance_m REAL,
  goal_quad_px TEXT NOT NULL,                         -- JSON [[x,y] x4]
  t_g_from_c TEXT NOT NULL,                           -- 4x4, frozen at the ARKit->AVF handoff
  reference_frame_path TEXT NOT NULL,                 -- 960x540 gray
  attitude_quat TEXT NOT NULL,                        -- q1 from the handoff
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE uploads (
  shot_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, upload_id TEXT, artifact_id TEXT,
  bytes INTEGER NOT NULL, committed_parts TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
  state TEXT NOT NULL, attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, next_attempt_at INTEGER
);

Migrations ship as a user_version pragma ladder.

6.7 The device / server split

Data Device Server Rationale
Raw shot video ✅ authoritative until uploaded, cached 14 days ✅ after upload Capture works with no network
Capture sidecar ✅ written at capture ✅ It is evidence about the capture; only the device can produce it
Field profiles, camera placements ✅ (reused next session offline) ✅ mirrored "Registered fields are reused next session" has to work with no signal
Shot rows + local lifecycle ✅ source of truth until upload ✅ source of truth after Only the device knows what it captured before it got signal; only the server knows what the pipeline said
ball_flight, results, GLB, rendered videos cached ✅ source of truth Produced by the workers
Benchmarks, cohorts, rankings, JK records cached (TTL 1 h) ✅ source of truth Cohort membership is a server judgement
Multi-shot coaching verdicts cached ✅ source of truth "6 of your last 7" is a cross-shot query with an N-of-M rule; never computed on device
SMPL/SMPL-X body model, GVHMR weights ❌ never ✅ Licensing and size
Auth tokens ✅ expo-secure-store (Keychain) — Scaffold rule

07 APIs and asynchronous job states

7.1 Conventions

  • Versioning is a URL prefix: /v1/…. (A Accept: application/vnd.stryke.v1+json scheme was considered and rejected: openapi-typescript generates from paths and does not model content negotiation, FastAPI has no built-in negotiation, and the committed contracts/openapi.json that CI diffs would not show a version change at all. A prefix is visible in the diff, in logs, and in the generated client.)
  • Errors are RFC 9457 Problem Details with a stable type: https://stryke.app/problems/{code}. §7.8 is the complete registry; a code not listed there may not be returned.
  • Every list endpoint is cursor-paginated: ?cursor=&limit= → {items, next_cursor}.
  • Response schemas live in the owning module's schemas.py, except the shared ones in stryke_api/schemas/shared.py.

7.2 Contracts v2 — the only be-api ↔ be-vision surface

CONTRACT_VERSION goes to "2"; be-vision must keep reading v1 for one release. The breaking parts of v2: JobStatus gains REFUSED and CANCELLED, AnalysisJob gains a required lane, and AnalysisResult replaces the flat shots list with ball + pose + render.

Resolved

Resolved: three "contracts v2" proposals existed. 04 §6's lane-aware shape wins on structure — it is the only one of the three that is lane-aware, and the two-lane split needs that. It absorbs 03 §1.5's contact_source, confidence_inputs, goal_crossing, mode, field_profile_id, shot_index and verified_eligible; 05 §3.2's Measured wrapper is not adopted on the wire, because Claims + _no_unclaimed_numbers + value-or-null-plus-reason encode the identical invariant on a flatter shape that maps 1:1 onto ball_flight.json. There is exactly one Measured and it is the client view type (§4.1); a server-side twin of the same name would guarantee a mapper bug. MeasurementGrade is likewise not in the contract — the serving grades are derived in be-api (§7.5), and be-vision does not know a user's mode is ranked. 03 §1.5 and 05 §3.2 each get a one-line "superseded by contracts v2" note.

CONTRACT_VERSION = "2"
SUPPORTED_CONTRACT_VERSIONS = frozenset({"1", "2"})

class JobStatus(StrEnum):
    QUEUED="queued"; RUNNING="running"; SUCCEEDED="succeeded"; FAILED="failed"
    REFUSED="refused"      # v2: ran fine, declined to produce numbers (input quality)
    CANCELLED="cancelled"  # v2: the user or the API withdrew it

class Lane(StrEnum):
    """BALL   pure-CPU OpenCV/NumPy — 'No GVHMR dependency; contact comes from ball kinematics'.
              What it needs instead is the FIELD PROFILE: in Ranked mode camera height, tilt and
              focal are fixed from T_G_from_C, which is why Ranked drops the fit from 7 free
              parameters to 3.
       POSE   needs an NVIDIA GPU (GVHMR + SMPL-X).
       RENDER needs the GPU and runs ON TAP only. It consumes the pose lane's params.npz.

       What the split does and does not buy: the trajectory, the arc, the placement and the band
       land without the GPU, so the shot map fills in while the pose lane is cold. The CONFIDENCE
       does NOT — c_pose is a hard multiplicand, so ranking eligibility is pose-gated even though
       the arc is not. That is what shot.status='partial' means."""
    BALL="ball"; POSE="pose"; RENDER="render"

class PipelineStage(StrEnum):
    INGEST="ingest"; STABILIZE="stabilize"; TRACK_BALL="track_ball"           # ball lane
    DETECT_CONTACT="detect_contact"; FIT_3D="fit_3d"
    PREPROCESS="preprocess"; MOTION="motion"; KINEMATICS="kinematics"         # pose lane
    RENDER="render"; EXPORT="export"                                          # render lane

STAGE_COUNT      = {Lane.BALL: 5, Lane.POSE: 3, Lane.RENDER: 2}
DEFAULT_DEADLINE_S = {Lane.BALL: 120, Lane.POSE: 600, Lane.RENDER: 300}
#   Per-lane, because a 480 s default on a 30 s job is a six-minute lease on nothing.

class ShotType(StrEnum):   FREE_KICK="free_kick"; DRIBBLE="dribble"; FIRST_TOUCH="first_touch"
class FailureClass(StrEnum):
    INPUT="input"                    # the clip is the problem — NEVER retried
    INFRASTRUCTURE="infrastructure"  # OOM, preempted, timeout — retried
    CONTRACT="contract"              # version mismatch — never retried, pages us

class ArtifactKind(StrEnum):
    CLIP_SHOT="clip_shot"; CLIP_PLAYBACK="clip_playback"; OVERLAY="overlay"; QA_STRIP="qa_strip"
    HERO_RENDER="hero_render"; SHARE_RENDER="share_render"; MESH_GLB="mesh_glb"
    MESH_PLY_ZIP="mesh_ply_zip"; PARAMS_NPZ="params_npz"; SETUP_FRAME="setup_frame"
    THUMBNAIL="thumbnail"; JK_LESSON="jk_lesson"; JK_CLIP="jk_clip"

class RefusalReason(StrEnum):
    TOO_FEW_DETECTIONS="too_few_detections"          # < 8 real post-contact detections
    NO_METRIC_REFERENCE="no_metric_reference";  TIMEBASE_UNTRUSTED="timebase_untrusted"
    RETIMED_SUSPECTED="retimed_suspected";      CONTACT_OCCLUDED="contact_occluded"
    CONTACT_DISAGREED="contact_disagreed";      BALL_TOO_SMALL="ball_too_small"
    CAMERA_MOTION_EXCEEDS_TOLERANCE="camera_motion_exceeds_tolerance"
    SOLVER_RAILED="solver_railed";              NO_KICKER_DETECTED="no_kicker_detected"
    KICKER_NOT_FULLY_IN_FRAME="kicker_not_fully_in_frame";  MULTIPLE_BALLS="multiple_balls"

class QualityFlag(StrEnum):
    CAMERA_PAN_COMPENSATED="camera_pan_compensated"; NO_GOAL_IN_FRAME="no_goal_in_frame"
    GOAL_FRONTO_PARALLEL="goal_fronto_parallel";     DOWN_THE_AXIS="down_the_axis"
    LOW_LIGHT="low_light"; DUPLICATE_FRAMES="duplicate_frames"; BASIN_AMBIGUOUS="basin_ambiguous"
    RAILED_PARAMETERS="railed_parameters"; SCREEN_RECORDING_SUSPECTED="screen_recording_suspected"
    CLIP_SHOT_LIMIT_EXCEEDED="clip_shot_limit_exceeded"      # imports only

Job inputs. No numeric field carries a non-null default anywhere in this file:

class GoalSpec(BaseModel):
    """Triple duty: metric scale, camera self-calibration, and the coordinate origin."""
    width_m: float = Field(gt=0); height_m: float = Field(gt=0)
    preset: str                              # full | futsal | mini | custom — required
    corners_px: list[dict] | None = None     # named image points; >=4, non-coplanar preferred
    non_coplanar: bool = False

class BallSpec(BaseModel):
    """No defaults. A size-5 220 mm ball assumed for a child's size-3 is a 14% diameter-ruler bias
    entering the fit AS A MEASUREMENT. An unset value must be a validation error at this boundary."""
    size: int = Field(ge=3, le=5); diameter_m: float = Field(gt=0)

class FieldGeometry(BaseModel):
    """Present iff mode == 'ranked'. This is what removes camera height and tilt from the parameter
    vector and kills the basin degeneracy."""
    field_profile_id: UUID
    t_g_from_c: list[list[float]]            # 4x4, goal frame from camera, this session
    ground_plane: dict
    closure_width_err_pct: float             # feeds c_cal; NOT optional
    closure_height_err_pct: float
    shot_zone: dict                          # polygon in G, for launch_in_zone
    nominal_distance_m: float

class CameraSpec(BaseModel):
    device_model: str; lens: str = "wide"
    intrinsics_source: str                   # required; no silent "diagonal_heuristic"
    fx: float | None = None; fy: float | None = None; cx: float | None = None; cy: float | None = None
    readout_us: int | None = None            # rolling-shutter row time, from QuickTime metadata
    exposure_us: int | None = None           # for blur-length de-biasing
    height_m: float | None = None; tilt_deg: float | None = None
    azimuth_deg: float | None = None         # angle off the shot line; 90 = side-on

class CaptureMetadata(BaseModel):
    nominal_fps: float; container_fps: float | None = None; duration_s: float
    width: int; height: int; captured_at: datetime
    capture_source: str                      # in_app | photo_library — required
    original_file: bool                      # false => screen recording / re-share — required

class RenderSpec(BaseModel):
    """lane == RENDER only."""
    style: str                               # hologram | mesh — ONE style, never two
    watermark: str | None = None
    #   "estimated" for a Quick-Mode shot. Set by be-api from shot.mode, NEVER by the client:
    #   the Estimated watermark is "not optional and not a settings toggle", which only holds if
    #   the server decides it.
    include_trajectory: bool = True
    include_speed_burn_in: bool = False      # the worker refuses this when claims.point_speed is false

class AnalysisJob(BaseModel):
    contract_version: str = CONTRACT_VERSION
    job_id: UUID; shot_id: UUID; session_id: UUID; user_id: UUID
    lane: Lane
    mode: str                                # quick | ranked — required
    idempotency_key: str; attempt: int = 1
    shot_index: int = 0                      # position inside an imported multi-shot clip
    priority: int = 100
    deadline_s: int                          # required; be-api passes DEFAULT_DEADLINE_S[lane]
    video_uri: str                           # signed; the worker fetches it itself
    output_prefix: str
    clip_window_s: tuple[float, float] | None = None   # IDR trim-lead + the exact fit window
    sidecar_uri: str | None = None           # per-frame K + PTS for up to 840 frames
    camera_count: int = Field(default=1, ge=1, le=2)
    capture: CaptureMetadata; camera: CameraSpec; ball: BallSpec
    goal: GoalSpec | None = None             # None => no metric reference => band only
    field: FieldGeometry | None = None       # None => quick mode, 7 free parameters
    player_height_cm: float | None = None    # backup ruler
    render: RenderSpec | None = None         # required iff lane == RENDER
    params_uri: str | None = None            # lane == RENDER: signed URI of params.npz

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _lane_inputs_present(self) -> "AnalysisJob":
        if self.lane is Lane.RENDER and (self.render is None or self.params_uri is None):
            raise ValueError("a render job needs a RenderSpec and a params_uri")
        if self.mode == "ranked" and self.field is None:
            raise ValueError("ranked mode without FieldGeometry cannot fix the camera")
        return self

Results. Claims is the gate downstream must read; never gate on the presence of a field:

class Claims(BaseModel):
    point_speed: bool = False
    apex: bool = False
    elevation: bool = False        # gates BOTH elevation_deg and azimuth_deg
    spin_magnitude: bool = False   # hard-wired false; kept so nobody re-adds it

class ConfidenceTerms(BaseModel):
    """Every one is a value or an EXPLICIT null — never a neutral default. A lane fills the terms
    it can see and nulls the rest; be-api composes the two scores once every lane is terminal,
    because c_pose and c_ref come from different lanes and no single lane can compute them."""
    c_ref: float | None = None; c_time: float | None = None; c_det: float | None = None
    c_rms: float | None = None; c_basin: float | None = None; c_anchor: float | None = None
    c_rail: float | None = None; c_frame: float | None = None; c_cal: float | None = None
    c_pose: float | None = None; c_kp: float | None = None; c_subj: float | None = None

class AnchorEstimate(BaseModel):
    """One metric ruler's opinion, with its own band. NEVER averaged into a single number."""
    kmh: float | None = None; band_kmh: tuple[float, float] | None = None; used: bool = False

class BasinSolution(BaseModel):
    """A camera geometry that survives the fit. Multiple survivors are REPORTED, not hidden."""
    rank: int; rms_px: float; speed_kmh_central: float
    elevation_deg: float; azimuth_deg: float | None = None; apex_m: float
    camera_height_m: float; camera_tilt_deg: float; effective_g_ms2: float

class TimeBase(BaseModel):
    source: str                       # container_metadata | pts | in_app_capture
    decoded_fps: float; pts_frame_rate: float | None = None
    duplicate_frame_ratio: float; pts_interval_iqr_ratio: float
    retimed_suspected: bool; container_rate_disagreement: bool
    trusted: bool; fps_uncertainty: float; point_value_allowed: bool
    warnings: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)

class BallFlight(BaseModel):
    frame: str                                 # "G" | "B" — required
    points_3d: list[TrajectoryPoint] = Field(default_factory=list)
    launch_point: TrajectoryPoint | None = None
    contact_frame: int | None = None
    contact_source: str                        # ball_departure|foot_kinematics|reconciled|disagreed
    contact_disagreement_ms: float | None = None
    n_detections: int; n_frames_tracked: int

    speed_band_kmh: tuple[float, float]        # ALWAYS present. THIS IS THE PRODUCT.
    speed_kmh: float | None = None             # point value, or None
    speed_refusal_reason: str | None = None
    per_anchor: dict[str, AnchorEstimate] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    #   keys: goal | diameter | motion_plane. NO "gravity" key.
    anchor_spread_pct: float | None = None

    elevation_deg: float | None = None; azimuth_deg: float | None = None
    apex_m: float | None = None; apex_refusal_reason: str | None = None
    carry_m: float | None = None
    distance_m: float | None = None            # hypot(X,Z) of launch_point, frame G only
    goal_crossing: GoalCrossing | None = None
    launch_in_zone: bool | None = None

    basin_ambiguous: bool
    basin_solutions: list[BasinSolution] = Field(default_factory=list)
    railed_parameters: list[dict] = Field(default_factory=list)

    fit_rms_px: float; fit_model: str; drag_model: str; magnus_dofs: int
    effective_g_ms2: float; free_parameters: int
    spin: SpinEstimate = SpinEstimate()        # qualitative only; no rate field exists
    timebase: TimeBase; camera_solution: dict = Field(default_factory=dict)
    metric_reference: bool
    claims: Claims = Claims()
    confidence_inputs: ConfidenceTerms = ConfidenceTerms()

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _no_unclaimed_numbers(self) -> "BallFlight":
        # a NUMBER without its CLAIM
        if self.speed_kmh is not None and not self.claims.point_speed:
